# PART 02 — TONE 2: SUNDAY AND WEEKDAY OFFICES (SATURDAY VESPERS THROUGH SATURDAY MATINS)

*On Saturday Evening, at Little Vespers.*

*On "Lord, I have cried," Resurrection Stichera of the Octoechos, 3, repeating the first.*

*Tone 2:*
Come, let us worship God the Word, begotten of the Father before the ages, Who was incarnate of the Virgin Mary; for having endured the Cross, He was delivered over unto burial, as He Himself willed; and having risen from the dead, He saved me, an erring man.[^1328]

Christ our Savior, having nailed unto the Cross the handwriting that was against us, blotted it out, and abolished the dominion of death; we worship His third-day resurrection.[^1329]

With the Archangels let us hymn the resurrection of Christ; for He is the Redeemer and Savior of our souls; and in fearful glory and mighty power He comes again to judge the world which He created.[^1330]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion Dogmatikon, Tone 2:*
O the great mystery! Beholding the marvels, I proclaim the Godhead; for Emmanuel opened the doors of nature as the Lover of mankind, yet broke not the keys of virginity as God; but in such wise came forth from the womb as He entered through the hearing; in such wise was incarnate as He was conceived; impassibly He entered, ineffably He came forth, according to the Prophet who saith: This gate shall be shut; no one shall pass through it, save only the Lord, the God of Israel, Who hath great mercy.[^1331]

*Then: O Gladsome Radiance. Afterward, the Prokeimenon: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty. (Thrice).*[^1332]

*Vouchsafe, O Lord. Afterward, we say not the litanies, but immediately sing the Aposticha:*[^1333]

*At the Aposticha.*
*Resurrection Sticheron, Tone 2:*
Thy resurrection, O Christ Savior, hath enlightened the whole universe, and Thou hast called back Thine own creation. O Almighty Lord, glory to Thee.[^1334]

*Other Stichera to the Most-holy Theotokos, in the same tone:*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "When from the Tree" (Є҆гда̀ ѿ дре́ва тѧ̀):*
*Verse: I will remember thy name in every generation and generation.*[^1335]
Joy of all that sorrow, and protectress of the wronged, and feeder of the hungry, comfort of the wandering, staff of the blind, visitation of the sick, shelter and intercessor of those in labor, and helper of the orphaned: Mother of God on high, thou art the All-pure One! Hasten, we pray, that thy servants may be saved.[^1336]

*Verse: Give ear, O daughter, and see, and incline thine ear.*[^1337]
No other refuge unto the Creator and Master have we acquired, save thee, O Bride of God! Cast us not away from thy fervent intercession, nor put to shame them that run with love unto thy protection, O Mother of our God! Hasten, display the wealth of thy mercy, and deliver us now from wrath.[^1338]

*Verse: The rich among the people shall entreat thy face.*[^1339]
The fear of manifold temptations and grievous misfortunes now encompass me, and the abyss of despair overwhelms me; the tempest of my transgressions sorely confounds me. Come swiftly and deliver me who am overwhelmed, O only haven of the faithful, and lead me up unto the harbor of the divine high calling.[^1340]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion, Tone 2:*
Who can worthily praise thee and call thee blessed, O Maiden and Bride of God, for the deliverance that hath come to the world through thee? Giving thanks, therefore, we cry unto thee, saying: Rejoice, thou who hast deified Adam and united things divided! Rejoice, thou who hast enlightened our race by the light-bearing resurrection of Thy Son and our God! For the Christian race blesses thee unceasingly.[^1341]

*Then: Now lettest Thou: Trisagion: and after Our Father: Troparion: When Thou didst descend: Theotokion: Beyond all thought: and the Dismissal.*[^1342]

*On Saturday at Great Vespers.*

*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 10 verses, and sing Resurrection Stichera of the Octoechos, 3, and Eastern (Anatolian) Stichera, 4, and of the Menaion, 3; or 4, or 6, if a Saint is celebrated.*[^1343]

*Resurrection Stichera, Tone 2:*
Come, let us worship God the Word, begotten of the Father before the ages, Who was incarnate of the Virgin Mary; for having endured the Cross, He was delivered over unto burial, as He Himself willed; and having risen from the dead, He saved me, an erring man.[^1344]

Christ our Savior, having nailed unto the Cross the handwriting that was against us, blotted it out, and abolished the dominion of death; we worship His third-day resurrection.[^1345]

With the Archangels let us hymn the resurrection of Christ; for He is the Redeemer and Savior of our souls; and in fearful glory and mighty power He comes again to judge the world which He created.[^1346]

*Other Stichera, Eastern (Anatolian), in the same tone:*
The angel proclaimed Thee, the crucified and buried Master, and said unto the women: Come, see where the Lord lay; for He is risen, as He said, as all-powerful. Therefore we worship Thee, the only Immortal One; O Life-giver, Christ, have mercy upon us.[^1347]

By Thy Cross Thou hast abolished the curse of the tree; by Thy burial Thou hast slain the dominion of death; and by Thy rising Thou hast enlightened the human race. Therefore we cry unto Thee: O Benefactor, Christ our God, glory to Thee.[^1348]

The gates of death opened unto Thee in fear, O Lord, and the gate-...[^1349]

...keepers of hades, beholding Thee, were terrified; for Thou didst shatter the brazen gates, and didst crush the iron bars, and didst lead us out of darkness and the shadow of death, and didst tear our bonds asunder.[^1350]

Singing a saving hymn, from our lips let us send up praise: Come, let us all fall down in the house of the Lord, saying: O Thou Who wast crucified upon the Tree, and art risen from the dead, and art in the bosom of the Father, cleanse our sins.[^1351]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion Dogmatikon, Tone 2:*
The shadow of the law passed away when grace arrived; for even as the bush was not consumed while burning, so, a Virgin, thou gavest birth, and a Virgin thou didst remain. In place of the pillar of fire, the Sun of righteousness hath shone forth; in place of Moses, Christ, the salvation of our souls.[^1352]

*Then the Entrance with the censer, O Gladsome Radiance, the Prokeimenon, and the litanies.*[^1353]

*At the Aposticha.*
*Resurrection Stichera, Tone 2:*
Thy resurrection, O Christ Savior, hath enlightened the whole universe, and Thou hast called back Thine own creation; O Almighty Lord, glory to Thee.[^1354]

*Other Stichera, Alphabetical:*
*Verse: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty.*[^1355]
By the Tree, O Savior, Thou hast abolished the curse of the tree; by Thy burial Thou hast slain the dominion of death, and by Thy rising Thou hast enlightened our race. Therefore we cry unto Thee: O Life-giver, Christ our God, glory to Thee.[^1356]

*Verse: For He hath established the world, which shall not be moved.*[^1357]
Nailed upon the Cross, O Christ, Thou didst change the beauty of creation; and the soldiers, showing inhumanity, pierced Thy side with a spear, and the Jews asked to seal the tomb, not knowing Thy power; but through the tender compassion of Thy mercies, Thou didst accept burial, and didst rise on the third day; O Lord, glory to Thee.[^1358]

*Verse: Holiness becomes Thy house, O Lord, unto length of days.*[^1359]
O Life-giver, Christ, Who willingly didst endure the Passion for the sake of mortals, and didst descend into hades as the Mighty One, snatching away them that awaited Thy coming as from a strong beast, Thou didst grant them to inhabit Paradise instead of hades. Therefore unto us also, who glorify Thy third-...[^1360]

...-day rising, grant cleansing of sins, and great mercy.[^1361]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O new marvel, surpassing all ancient marvels! For who hath known a Mother to bear without a husband, and carry in her arms Him Who upholds all creation? It is the counsel of God that was born. Whom, as an Infant, O All-pure One, having carried in thine arms, and having motherly boldness toward Him, cease not entreating for them that honor thee, to have compassion and to save our souls.[^1362]

*Then: Now lettest Thou: Trisagion: and after Our Father:*
*Resurrectional Troparion, Tone 2:*
When Thou didst descend unto death, O Life Immortal, then didst Thou slay hades with the brilliance of the Godhead; and when Thou didst also raise the dead from the nethermost parts, all the heavenly hosts cried aloud: O Life-giver, Christ our God, glory to Thee.[^1363]

*Theotokion:*
All beyond thought, all most glorious are thy mysteries, O Theotokos! Sealed in purity and preserved in virginity, thou wast known to be a true Mother, having given birth unto the true God; Him entreat, that our souls may be saved.[^1364]

*On Saturday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most-holy Theotokos.*
*(Which let them also sing who are suffering from slander, in sorrows and misfortunes).*[^1365]
*Tone 2. Ode 1. Heirmos:* An over-armed power once cast down the whole army of Pharaoh into the deep; but the incarnate Word destroyed all-wicked sin, the most-glorious Lord, for gloriously is He glorified.[^1366]

*Refrain: Most-holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^1367]
*Troparion 1:* Thou who art readily obedient unto all in affliction, and helpest in sorrow, O good Theotokos, grant grace unto them that dare to sing of thee, O Sovereign Lady, Joy of all that sorrow.[^1368]

*Troparion 2:* Having acquired exceedingly rich grace, O Sovereign Lady, by thy bold prayer deliver me most gloriously from misfortunes, an impoverished servant of thine, O Joy of all that sorrow.[^1369]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* From visible and invisible enemies deliver them that flee unto thee, we pray, O Theotokos, and destroy every counsel of them that war against us.[^1370]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Take away the reproach of men and the slanders of plotters from me, O Theotokos, I pray thee, that I may fervently glorify the Lord, Whom thou didst nourish.[^1371]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Establish us in Thee, O Lord, Who by the Tree didst put sin to death, and plant Thy fear in the hearts of us who sing unto Thee.[^1372]

*Troparion 1:* Dispel the worldly plots of enemies, O Theotokos, and fail not through thy prayers to preserve us who praise thee.[^1373]

*Troparion 2:* Look down, O Pure One, with thy merciful eye, and deliver me from every plot of visible and invisible enemies, blinding the pupils of their eyes.[^1374]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Quench the evil assault of the enemies, burning like fire, who seek ever to destroy us, O Virgin, by the dew of thy prayers.[^1375]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Unquenchable lamp, dawn that shines ever: enlighten me who am held fast in the night of sorrows by thy prayers, O thou who didst bear Christ, the Sun of glory.[^1376]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I heard, O Lord, Thy voice, which Thou didst say: The voice of one crying in the wilderness, for Thou didst thunder over many waters, bearing witness to Thy Son; filled wholly with the descending Spirit, he cried aloud: Thou art Christ, the Wisdom and Power of God.[^1377]

*Troparion 1:* Thee, the bridge of salvation, the ungrudging prayer, the firm intercession, do we entreat: have compassion and behold our sorrow, sicknesses, afflictions, and passions; and graciously visit us, O Mother of God, granting swift joy.[^1378]

*Troparion 2:* We are not bereft of thy protection in sorrows, O Sovereign Lady; therefore even now help us swiftly, stretching forth a hand unto us who are sorely overwhelmed, O Pure One. Be merciful unto our infirmities, O Mother of God, granting swift joy.[^1379]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* The lawless hoped not in thee, O Sovereign Lady, but hoped in a boastful tongue, in the tongue of men that pours out envy continually, roaring to shed the blood of the neighbor unjustly; but do thou, O Pure One, shatter their jaws.[^1380]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Bring down, O Sovereign Lady, the haughty neck of the enemies who speak great things, their counsels and malice, and their hearts that meditate evils against me all the day; and strength and vic-...[^1381]

...-tory grant unto them that call upon thee, O Mother of God, granting swift joy.[^1382]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* The Coal shown forth unto Isaiah, the Sun from the virginal womb hath shone forth, granting the enlightenment of the knowledge of God unto them that wandered in darkness.[^1383]

*Troparion 1:* True intercessor, hope of Christians, O Full of grace, receive supplication from us who diligently call upon thee, and make prayer unto thee.[^1384]

*Troparion 2:* Knowing thee, O Pure One, to be the fountain of life that wellest forth the waters of immortality, we who are born of earth call thee blessed.[^1385]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Craftily hath the enemy armed himself against us, wishing to destroy us with his tongue as with a sword; but come swiftly, O Mother of God, with thy strength.[^1386]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Who can count the abyss of the intercessions of thy power, O Pure One? Therefore come swiftly unto us who are in distress.[^1387]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Wallowing in the abyss of sin, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy compassion: from corruption, O God, bring me up.[^1388]

*Troparion 1:* Being the mediatress of chastity, appear now unto them that call upon thee, and deliver us from all misfortunes and tribulations, O Bride of God.[^1389]

*Troparion 2:* Destroy the wicked deeds of the enemy, and stop unjust slanders, O blessed and all-pure one, delivering the innocent from affliction.[^1390]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Encompassed by grievous sins, and overwhelmed by calamitous misfortunes, we flee under thy divine protection, O Mother of Christ God.[^1391]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Having given birth unto the Lord without knowing man, thou didst appear after birthgiving still remaining a virgin. O most glorious marvel wrought in thee, O Bride of God![^1392]

*Then: Lord, have mercy, thrice.*
*Glory... Both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:*
Fervent prayer, and invincible wall, fountain of mercy, haven of the world: diligently do we cry unto thee: O Theotokos and Sovereign Lady, come swiftly, and deliver us from tribulations, O thou who alone art our speedy defender.[^1393]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* That Thou mightest clearly prefigure Thy most glorious birthgiving from the Virgin, O Christ, unconsumed...[^1394]

*Heirmos (continued):* ...preserved the Children in the furnace, as they sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^1395]

*Troparion 1:* O tender compassion of thine, O pure Virgin! for thou loosest boundless sorrows and misfortunes for them that call upon thee in need and distress. Therefore even now help them that praise thee, O Blessed One.[^1396]

*Troparion 2:* Show thy speedy protection; show that thou art able, as the Mother of God, for we call upon thee from the heart, falling down with tears: appease sorrow and sickness of thy servants speedily.

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* The mouths of men, like those of fierce lions, have opened wider than the grave, bitterly to swallow me down; but do thou, O Mother of God, appear as the hope of the hopeless, and cast down their strength, O Blessed One.

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Let the enemies see and be put to shame, let them understand and see thy power that fights against them on our behalf; and into the nethermost pit cast them down, O Blessed One, hope of the hopeless.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Of old the fiery furnace in Babylon divided its operation by the divine command, consuming the Chaldeans, but bedewing the faithful as they sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^1397]

*Troparion 1:* Our refuge and the joy of the world, O Mother of God, make haste to have compassion, and speedily grant thy grace unto us who are afflicted, and protect thy servants, O Good One.

*Troparion 2:* A vain counsel have the assemblies of the godless wickedly taken against us, even as of old Ahithophel. But we cry aloud: By thy prayers destroy this counsel, casting down their strength, O Mother of God.[^1398]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Unto them that call upon thee from the soul without deceit, O Mother of God, in every sorrow, in diverse sicknesses, and in fierce tribulations, give ear speedily, O Sovereign Lady, delivering them ever by thy prayers.

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Let thy name, O Mother of God, be glorified upon earth; for He Who shone forth from thee hath given thee as a mighty hope and a wall unto sinners; for through thee doth every breath hasten unto God.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Every tongue is at a loss to praise thee worthily, and even an intellect from above the world is dazed to hymn thee, O Mother of God! Yet being good, accept our faith, for thou...[^1399]

*Heirmos (continued):* ...knowest our divine love; for thou art the protectress of Christians, and thee do we magnify.[^1400]

*Troparion 1:* Let every tongue be stopped that meditates deceit; let deceitful lips be silent, and mouths that speak iniquity against the righteous man through pride and hostile envy together, by the prayer of the Mother of God and of the Saints of Christ.

*Troparion 2:* Watchful in prayers, we all call upon the Mother of God when held fast by sickness and sorrow, crying aloud: O pure Sovereign Lady, speedily deliver thy servants ever from the sicknesses that hold them fast, for after God we have no other protectress.

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* The great refuge of the despairing, the calm haven of the storm-tossed art thou, O Mother of God. Therefore we also hasten unto thee, crying aloud: Let us not be put to shame, O Mother of true Life, but thanking thee, let us magnify thee with fervor.

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Receive, O all-pure Maiden, this divine hymn, rendering grace unto them that hope in thee; and ask that peace be ever sent unto the churches, and unto our Emperor victories and triumphs, that every Christian tongue may magnify thee.[^1401]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office:*
*Triadic Canon.*
*(A creation of Metrophanes, whose acrostic is: I sing the Triadic light of the Godhead).*[^1402]

*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:* In the deep of old, the overwhelming power laid low the whole army of Pharaoh; and the incarnate Word destroyed all-wicked sin, the most glorified Lord; for gloriously hath He been glorified.[^1403]

*Refrain:* Most Holy Trinity our God, glory to Thee.

*Troparion 1:* The Triadic and single-principled nature of the Godhead let us hymn in song, saying: Having essentially an inexhaustible abyss of mercy, preserve and save them that bow down before Thee, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion 2:* Being the fountain and root, the Father, as the Cause of the consubstantial Godhead in the Son and in Thy Holy Spirit, pour forth tri-radiant light unto my heart, and illuminate me with the participation of divine radiance.

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* O tri-radiant Unity of divine origin, destroy the whole darkness of my sins and passions by the sweetest communions of Thy bright rays, and make...

*Troparion 3 (continued):* ...me a pure temple and tabernacle of Thine unapproachable glory.[^1404]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
The ancient stream of our nature, which suffered unseemly and slipped into corruption, O all-pure one, God the Word, having become incarnate in thy womb, illuminated in His love for mankind, and mystically taught us the tri-radiant divine principle.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Having established me upon the rock of faith, Thou hast enlarged my mouth against mine enemies. For my spirit hath rejoiced to sing: None is holy like our God, and none is righteous save Thee, O Lord.[^1405]

*Troparion 1:* By equality of nature, O Godhead of single honor, I glorify Thee in Persons; for Thou, proceeding as Life from Life without corruption, art our one God, and none is holy save Thee, O Lord.

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast constituted the immaterial and heavenly ranks as mirrors of Thy goodness, O Trinity, indivisible single sovereignty, to hymn Thee unceasingly. But receive now also our praise from lips of clay.

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Establish upon the rock of faith, and enlarge with the abyss of Thy love, the hearts and minds of Thy servants, O Unity of three suns; for Thou art our God, hoping in Whom may we not be put to shame.

*Both now... Theotokion:*
He Who before substantiated all the essence of creation was substantiated in thy womb through unreckonable goodness, O Mother of God, and the tri-radiant light of the one Godhead and Lordship shone forth unto all.

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 2, Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Благоꙋтро́бїа):*[^1406]
When in the beginning Thou didst fashion Adam, O Lord, then didst Thou cry unto Thine hypostatic Word, O Compassionate One: Let Us make him according to Our likeness; and the Holy Spirit was present as Co-Creator. Therefore we cry unto Thee: O Creator, our God, glory to Thee!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
When God was pleased to come unto us, then did He take up His abode in thy most pure womb, O all-pure one, and through thee saved the human mixture, granting unto all the heavenly kingdom. Therefore we cry unto thee, O pure Mother of God: Rejoice, O Sovereign Lady!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I sing unto Thee; for by report, O Lord, have I heard and was afraid; for Thou comest unto me, seeking...[^1407]

*Heirmos (continued):* ...me who was lost. Therefore I glorify Thy great condescension toward me, O Many-merciful One.[^1408]

*Troparion 1:* Not even the immaterial Angelic ranks can comprehend Thee, O Unity, unoriginate Trinity! But we with clay tongues hymn Thine essential goodness, and glorify Thee with faith.

*Troparion 2:* Being the Creator of human nature, O Almighty, Thou seest all mine infirmity now as the All-seeing One; therefore show mercy unto Thy servant, and lead me again unto the better life.

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* We hymn the three unconfused Persons of the originating Unity, as having proper and distinct Hypostases; yet united and indivisible in counsel, and glory, and Godhead.

*Both now... Theotokion:*
A pure and blameless temple, O Ever-virgin Mother of God, the Fashioner of all found thee alone manifested from all ages; and having taken up His abode in thee, He refashioned human nature, as the Lover of mankind.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Enlightenment of them that lie in darkness, salvation of the despairing, O Christ my Savior, unto Thee I rise early, O King of peace; enlighten me with Thine illumination, for I know no other God save Thee.[^1409]

*Troparion 1:* As Thou extendest in every way the peace-bestowing and saving rays of Thy providence over all existing things, O King of peace, preserve me in Thy peace; for Thou art life and peace unto all.

*Troparion 2:* As Thou didst appear unto Moses in the bush in a fiery vision, Thou wast named the Angel, O Word of the Father, prefiguring Thine advent unto us; whereby Thou hast clearly proclaimed unto all the tri-hypostatic dominion of the one Godhead.[^1410]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Having set forth Thy natural, co-everlasting glory, O most sovereign Holy Trinity, vouchsafe them that hymn Thee with orthodox faith to behold Thy glory, the unoriginate and single radiance of three suns.

*Both now... Theotokion:*
He Who by essence is God the Word, the Container of all ages, was incomprehensibly contained in thy womb, O Virgin Mother, calling men unto the unity of the one Lordship.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Wallowing in the abyss of sin, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy compassion: from corruption, O God, bring me up.[^1411]

*Troparion 1:* O Lover of mercy, have mercy upon them that believe in Thee, O God of three suns, and deliver Thy servants from transgressions, and passions, and tribulations. *(Twice)*

*Glory...*
*Troparion 2:* By the ineffable abyss of goodness, grant unto me the light-giving ray of Thine unsearchable radiance and tri-radiant Godhead.

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Ineffably, O Virgin, the Most High became man from thee, having clothed Himself in man in all things; and with tri-radiant light did He illuminate me.

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 2, Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Благоꙋтро́бїа):*[^1412]
Having extended unto us the abyss of compassion, receive us, O Merciful One. Look down upon the people that glorify Thee, receive the hymn of them that beseech Thee, O Trinity, unoriginate Unity; for in Thee, the God of all, do we hope, that Thou mayest grant forgiveness of transgressions.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Having given birth unto the fountain of compassion, thou art merciful, O good Mother of God; for thou art the only protection of the faithful, thou art the consolation of the sorrowful. Therefore unto thee do we all now fall down in faith, that we may find deliverance from afflictions, being enriched by thine only help.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Unto the golden image served in the plain of Dura, Thy three Youth paid no heed to the godless decree; but cast into the midst of the fire, bedewed they sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^1413]

*Troparion 1:* Thou ever ordainest the Angelic hosts unto unchangeableness, O Lord Who alone art immutable and tri-hypostatic! Show therefore mine heart also ever unchangeable, that I may warmly glorify Thee and piously hymn Thee. *(Twice)*

*Glory...*
*Troparion 2:* The noetic choirs of immaterial essences, illumined by Thy rays, O God of single sovereignty and three suns, become secondary lights by adoption. Illumine me also with their radiances and participation, and show me to be light, as the tri-radiant Author of light.

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Fail not to guide us and to raise us up to heaven, who love thee, for through ineffable...

*Theotokion (continued):* ...love for mankind He became man in the womb of the Virgin, and having deified man, sits with the Father upon the throne of glory.[^1414]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Disregarding the golden image, the thrice-blessed youths, seeing the unchanging and living image of God, sang amid the fire: Let all creation that hath been substantiated praise the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.[^1415]

*Troparion 1:* O unapproachable Trinity, co-ever-existing, co-unoriginate, divine first principle, immutable in all things save light-bearing properties: make void every wicked counsel of adversaries and tribulations of demons, preserving me ever unharmed, O Lord of all.

*Troparion 2:* O uncircumscribed, tri-radiant single sovereignty, having composed the world wisely and almighty, and preserving it in unharmed, all-perfect order: take up Thine abode in my heart, to sing and glorify Thee unceasingly with the Angelic choirs unto all ages.

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* O Wisdom of the Father, incomprehensible, ineffable Word of God, without altering Thy changeless nature, Thou didst mercifully assume human nature, and hast taught all to honor the unique Trinity as the Lordship of all ages.

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Righteousness and Wisdom, Which guides all things, loved thee worthily, O all-honored and light-bearing Virgin, and ineffably took up His abode in thee, O all-pure one; Whom singing in hymnody, we sing, crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing and exalt Him above all unto the ages.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Thee who ineffably didst incarnate from the virginal side God the luminary, Who shone forth before the sun and came to us in the flesh, O blessed all-pure Mother of God, do we magnify.[^1416]

*Troparion 1:* From the unoriginate Light the Son, co-unoriginate Light, shone forth, and the consubstantial Light the Spirit proceeded; ineffably, in a manner befitting God, an incorruptible begetting is confirmed, together with an ineffable procession.

*Troparion 2:* Shine forth in the hearts of them that hymn Thee with Thy tri-radiant light, O Godhead of three suns; and grant understanding that we may perceive in all things and do Thy will, good and perfect, and magnify and glorify Thee.

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Being unsearchable by nature as God, having an unsearchable abyss of compassions...

*Troparion 3 (continued):* ...as Thou didst show compassion of old, O Trinity: so now also have compassion on Thy servants, and deliver us from transgressions, and from afflictions and tribulations.[^1417]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Save me, O my God, from every temptation and affliction, Who in three Persons art ineffably hymned as the only God and Almighty; and preserve Thy flock by the prayers of the Mother of God.[^1418]

*On Sunday at Matins,*
*At "God is the Lord," Troparion:*
When Thou didst descend... (see fol. 132)[^1419]
*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
All beyond understanding... (see fol. 132)[^1420]

*After the 1st Kathisma, Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 2:*
The noble Joseph, having taken down Thine all-pure Body from the Tree, wrapped it in a clean plashchanytsia with sweet spices, and laying it in a new tomb, closed it; but on the third day Thou didst arise, O Lord, granting great mercy unto the world.[^1421]

*Verse:* Arise, O Lord my God, let Thine hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.[^1422]

Refusing not that the stone of the tomb be sealed, having arisen, Thou didst grant the rock of faith unto all: O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Most glorified art thou, O Virgin Theotokos, we hymn thee; for by the Cross of thy Son hades was overthrown, and death was put to death; we who were dead have arisen and been deemed worthy of life: we have received paradise, the ancient enjoyment. Therefore, giving thanks, we glorify Him as mighty Christ our God, and the only Many-merciful One.

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 2:*
Standing at the tomb, the Angel cried unto the Myrrh-bearing women: Myrrh is fitting for the dead, but Christ hath appeared a stranger to corruption. But cry aloud: The Lord is risen, granting great mercy unto the world.[^1423]

*Verse:* I will confess unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart, I will tell of all Thy marvelous works.[^1424]

The choir of Thy disciples rejoices in harmony with the Myrrh-bearing women; for a common feast do they celebrate with them, unto the glory and honor of Thy Resurrection. For their sake we cry unto Thee, O Lord, Lover of mankind: Unto Thy people grant great mercy.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Concealed from the laws of nature by a divine birthgiving, thou didst combine virginity; for thou alone didst give birth unto Him Who was begotten timelessly before thee. Therefore, O Mother of God, we magnify thee.[^1425]

*After the Blameless, the Hypakoe, Tone 2:*
Going unto the tomb after the Passion to anoint Thy Body, O Christ God, the women saw Angels in the tomb and were dismayed; for they heard a voice from them: The Lord is risen, granting great mercy unto the world.[^1426]

*The Hymns of Ascent, Tone 2.*
*Antiphon 1.*
Unto heaven do I direct the eyes of my heart, unto Thee, O Savior! Save me by Thine illumination.

Have mercy upon us who sin against Thee much at every hour, O my Christ! and give a pattern of repentance unto Thee before the end.

*Glory...*
Unto the Holy Spirit it appertains to reign, to sanctify, and to animate creation; for He is God, consubstantial with the Father and the Word.
*Both now... the same.*

*Antiphon 2.*
If the Lord had not been among us, who could have been kept whole from the enemy, who is at once both a murderer of man?

Unto their teeth surrender not Thy servant, O Savior; for like lions do mine enemies move against me.

*Glory...*
Unto the Holy Spirit belongs life-originating power and honor; for as being God, He preserves all created things by His power in the Father and through the Son.
*Both now... the same.*

*Antiphon 3.*
They that trust in the Lord are like the holy mountain, which is in no wise moved by the assaults of the enemy.

Let not them that live godly stretch forth their hands in iniquity; for Christ leaves not the rod upon His lot.

*Glory...*
By the Holy Spirit all wisdom is welled forth; thence is grace unto the Apostles, and by sufferings the Martyrs are crowned, and the Prophets behold.
*Both now... the same.*

*Prokeimenon, Tone 2:*
Arise, O Lord my God, by the decree wherewith Thou hast commanded, and a congregation of peoples shall surround Thee.[^1427]

*Verse:* O Lord my God, in Thee have I hoped; save me.[^1428]

*Let every breath... The Sunday Gospel. Having seen the Resurrection of Christ... Psalm 50, and the rest.*

*Resurrectional Canon.*
*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:* In the deep of old, the overwhelming power laid low the whole army of Pharaoh; and the incarnate Word destroyed all-wicked sin, the most glorified Lord; for gloriously hath He been glorified.[^1429]

*Refrain:* Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.

*Troparion 1:* The prince of this world, O Good One, under whom we were enrolled because we did not listen to Thy commandment, was condemned by Thy Cross; for he assaulted Thee as mortal, but fell away from the might of Thy power, and his weakness was reproved.

*Troparion 2:* The Redeemer of the human race, and the Prince of incorruptible life, Thou camest into the world, and by Thy Resurrection didst tear asunder the swaddling-bands of death, which we all glorify; for gloriously hath He been glorified.

*Theotokion:* Thou hast appeared higher, O pure Ever-virgin, than all invisible and visible creation; for thou didst give birth unto the Creator, even as He was well pleased to become incarnate in thy womb. Him do thou entreat with boldness, to save our souls.

*Another Canon, Cross-Resurrectional.*
*(Whose acrostic is: I sing praise to the Life-bearing Word).*[^1430]
*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:* Traversing an untrodden, unusual path through the sea without wetness, the chosen Israel cried aloud: Let us sing unto the Lord, for He is glorified.[^1431]

*Refrain:* Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Resurrection.

*Troparion 1:* The power of the weak, the resurrection of the fallen, and the incorruption of the dead hast Thou become, O Christ, by the passion of Thy flesh; for He is glorified.

*Troparion 2:* Have compassion upon the fallen image, and resurrect that which is broken, O Creator God and Renewer; being put to death, quicken all; for He is glorified.

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*(Whose acrostic is: I sing praise to the Life-bearing Maiden).*[^1432]
*Tone 2. The same Heirmos:*
The immaterial ladder of old, and the path of the sea strangely turned to stone, manifested thy birthgiving, O Pure One, which we all sing; for He is glorified.

*Troparion 1:* The power of the Most High, the perfect Hypostasis, the Wisdom of God, having become incarnate from thee, O all-pure one, conversed with men; for He is glorified.

*Troparion 2:* The Sun of righteousness passed through the impassable door of thy sealed womb, O Pure One, and shone forth unto the world; for He is glorified.[^1433]

*Katavasia:* I will open my mouth... (see fol. 15)[^1434]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* The wilderness hath blossomed like a lily, O Lord, the barren church of the nations, by Thy coming, wherein my heart hath been established.[^1435]

*Troparion 1:* Creation was changed at Thy Passion, beholding Thee in poor appearance mocked by the lawless, Who didst establish all things by divine nod.

*Troparion 2:* From the dust according to Thine image Thou didst fashion me with Thine hand, and when I was crushed again into the dust of death because of sin, O Christ, descending into hades, Thou didst raise me up with Thyself.

*Theotokion:* The Angelic ranks were amazed, O all-pure one, and the hearts of men were terrified at thy birthgiving; therefore, as the Mother of God in faith do we honor thee.

*Another, Heirmos:* The bow of the mighty is broken by Thy might, O Christ, and with power the weak have girded themselves.[^1436]

*Troparion 1:* Christ, Who is higher than all, was made a little lower than the angelic nature through bodily suffering.

*Troparion 2:* Counted dead among the lawless, shining with the crown of glory, O Christ, Thou didst appear unto the women from the resurrection.

*Another, the same Heirmos:*
He Who is above all time, as the Creator of time, was willingly fashioned as an Infant from thee, O Virgin.

*Troparion 1:* Let us hymn the womb more strange than the heavens, through which Adam lives rejoicing in the heavens.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Thou didst come forth from the Virgin, not an intercessor nor an Angel, but Thyself incarnate, O Lord, and hast saved me, the whole man. Therefore I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^1437]

*Troparion 1:* Thou standest before the judgment-seat as One judged, O my God, not crying aloud, O Master, bringing judgment unto the nations; whereby in Thy Passion, O Christ, Thou hast wrought salvation for the universe.

*Troparion 2:* By Thy Passion, O Christ, the weapons of the enemy have failed; and by Thine adverse descent into hades cities were destroyed, and the boldness of the tyrant was cast down.[^1438]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1439]

*Theotokion:* Thee, as a haven of salvation and an unshakable wall, O Lady Theotokos, all we faithful know; for by Thy prayers Thou deliverest our souls from afflictions.

*Another Heirmos:*[^1440] I heard, O Lord, Thy glorious dispensation, and I glorified Thy incomprehensible power, O Lover of mankind.

*Troparion:* Beholding Thee nailed upon the Tree, O Christ, the Virgin, who without travail gave birth to Thee, endured maternal pangs.

*Troparion:* Death has been conquered; a Dead Man plundered the gates of hades; for the all-devourer being destroyed, all things above nature have been bestowed upon me.

*Another Heirmos the same:*[^1441] Lo, the divine mountain of the house of the Lord has clearly been exalted above the powers, as the Mother of God.

*Troparion:* Apart from natural laws, O Virgin, alone having given birth to Him Who rules over creation, Thou wast vouchsafed the divine calling.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^1442] A Mediator between God and men hast Thou become, O Christ God; for through Thee, O Master, we have access from the night of ignorance unto Thy Father, the Author of light.

*Troparion:* Like the cedars, O Christ, Thou didst crush the raging of the nations, O Master, by Thy will (as Thou wast well-pleased), being bodily lifted up upon the cypress, the pine, and the cedar.[^1443]

*Troparion:* In the lowest pit they laid Thee, O Christ, a breathless corpse; but by Thy wound, O Wounded One, Thou didst raise up together with Thyself the forgotten dead who slept in the tombs.

*Theotokion:* Entreat Thy Son and Lord, O pure Virgin, to grant peace unto them that hope in Thee, and deliverance unto the captives from adverse assaults.

*Another Heirmos:*[^1444] The live coal revealed unto Isaiah, the Sun, shone forth from the virginal womb, granting the illumination of divine knowledge unto them that wandered in darkness.

*Troparion:* Having refused to fast, the first Adam tasted of the deadly tree; but He that was crucified, the Second [Adam], destroys his sin.

*Troparion:* In human nature Thou wast both passible and mortal, Who art impassible in immaterial Divinity; having incorruptible life, O Christ, Thou didst raise the dead from the lowest depths of hades.

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1445]

*Another Heirmos the same:*[^1446] Ye clouds, drop down sweetness of gladness upon them that are on the earth; for a Child is given, Who is before the ages, having become incarnate of the Virgin, our God.

*Troparion:* Light has shone forth upon my life and flesh, and destroyed the gloomy sorrow of sin, when in the latter days the Most High became incarnate without seed of the Virgin.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^1447] Wallowing in the abyss of sin, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy compassion: from corruption, O God, raise Thou me up!

*Troparion:* As a malefactor the Righteous One was condemned, and with the lawless He was nailed unto the Tree, by His blood granting remission unto the guilty.

*Troparion:* By one man, the first Adam, of old did death enter into the world, and by one, the Son of God, has resurrection been revealed.

*Theotokion:* Without experience of man, O Virgin, didst Thou give birth, and Thou abidest a virgin, showing forth the images of the true Godhead of Thy Son and God.

*Another Heirmos:*[^1448] Having heard the voice of prayerful words from an afflicted soul, O Master, deliver me from evil plights; for Thou alone art the author of our salvation.

*Troparion:* Thou didst set the Cherubim as guardians of the tree of life for fallen [man], but seeing Thee, the gates opened; for Thou didst appear, making a way into Paradise for the thief.[^1449]

*Troparion:* Hades was emptied and overthrown by the death of One; for the abundant wealth which it had treasured up, Christ, the One on behalf of us all, has poured out.

*Another Heirmos the same:*[^1450] Human nature that was in servitude to sin, O pure Lady, has attained freedom through Thee; for Thy Son, as a Lamb, is slain on behalf of all.

*Troparion:* We all cry unto Thee, the true Mother of God: deliver Thy servants who have provoked [Him] to anger; for Thou alone hast boldness toward the Son.

*Kontakion, Tone 2:*[^1451] Thou didst rise from the tomb, O All-powerful Savior! and the dead arose; and creation, beholding it, rejoices with Thee, and Adam is glad, and the world, O my Savior, hymns Thee ever.

*Ikos:*[^1452] Thou art the Light of the darkened, Thou art the Resurrection of all and Life *On Sunday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1453]

*Ikos (continued):* ...of men; and Thou didst raise all together with Thyself, having destroyed the dominion of death, O Savior, and having crushed the gates of hades, O Word! And the dead, seeing the marvel, were amazed, and every creature rejoices in Thy Resurrection, O Lover of mankind. Therefore we also all glorify and hymn Thy condescension, and the world, O my Savior, hymns Thee ever.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^1454] The God-opposing decree of the lawless tyrant raised a lofty flame; but Christ spread spiritual dew upon the pious children, He Who is blessed and supremely praised.

*Troparion:* Out of Thy tender compassion, O Master, Thou didst not endure to see man tormented by death; but coming, Thou didst save him by Thy blood, having become man, O blessed and supremely praised God of our fathers.

*Troparion:* Seeing Thee, O Christ, clad in the garment of vengeance, the gatekeepers of hades were terrified; for Thou, O Master, didst come to slay the servant of the mindless tyrant, O blessed and supremely praised God of our fathers.[^1455]

*Theotokion:* We know Thee to be the Holy of Holies, as the only one that gavest birth to the immutable God, O undefiled Virgin, unwedded Mother! For unto all the faithful Thou didst pour forth incorruption by Thy divine birthgiving.

*Another Heirmos:*[^1456] The youths of old appeared as most wise orators; for theologizing from a God-receptive soul, with their lips they sang: O supremely divine God of our fathers and our God, blessed art Thou.

*Troparion:* Of old disobedience condemned the forefather in Eden; but willingly being judged, He looses the transgressions of the transgressor, the supremely divine God of our fathers, and supremely praised.

*Troparion:* Thou didst save him who was wounded by the tongue [of the serpent], through the envy of the murderer of men, by willful biting in Eden; for by Thy voluntary Passion Thou didst heal him, O supremely divine God of our fathers, and supremely praised.

*Theotokion:* Walking in the shadow of death, Thou didst summon me unto the light, having compassed dark hades with the radiance of Godhead, O supremely divine God of our fathers, and supremely praised.

*Another Heirmos the same:*[^1457] Jacob indeed beheld in the night, as in an enigma, God incarnate; and from Thee has He appeared with brightness *On Sunday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1458]

*Another Heirmos the same (continued):* ...unto them that sing: O supremely divine God of our fathers, and supremely praised.

*Troparion:* Foreshowing the tokens of the ineffable union in Thee, He wrestled with Jacob, through which He willingly united Himself unto men, O Pure One, the supremely divine God of our fathers, and supremely praised.

*Troparion:* Abominable is he who with unhesitating faith proclaims Thee not to be the Son of the Virgin, One of the most-praised Trinity, and crying with his tongue: O supremely divine God of our fathers, and supremely praised.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^1459] The fiery furnace of old in Babylon divided its actions, by the divine decree burning the Chaldeans, but moistening the faithful with dew, as they sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Troparion:* Beholding the garment of Thy flesh dyed red with Thy blood, O Christ, the Angelic ranks were amazed with trembling at Thy great longsuffering, crying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^1460]

*Troparion:* Thou didst clothe my mortal nature in immortality by Thy rising, O Compassionate One. Therefore, rejoicing, the chosen people gratefully hymn Thee, O Christ, crying unto Thee: Death in truth has been swallowed up in victory![^1461]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst conceive without seed Him Who is inseparable from the Father, Who dwelt divinely-humanly in Thy womb, and ineffably gavest birth, O most pure Parent of God; therefore we know Thee to be the salvation of us all.

*Another Heirmos:*[^1462] Disregarding the golden image, the thrice-blessed youths, seeing the immutable and living image of God, sang in the midst of the fire: Let all existing creation praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Troparion:* Thou wast seen nailed upon the Cross, Who art rich in mercy; willingly wast Thou buried, and on the third day didst rise, and didst deliver all men, O Lover of mankind, who sing in faith: Let all creation praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Troparion:* Deliver from corruption, having descended into the nether parts, O Word of God, Him whom Thou didst fashion, O Christ, by Thy divine power; and having made Him free from corruption, Thou didst make Him a partaker of Thine ever-existing glory: Let all creation, crying, praise and supremely exalt Christ unto all ages!

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1463]

*Another Heirmos the same:*[^1464] He was seen upon earth through Thee, and dwelt among men, Who is incomparable in goodness and might. Singing unto Him, all we faithful cry: Let all existing creation praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Troparion:* Truly proclaiming Thee pure, we glorify Thee, the Theotokos; for Thou gavest birth to One of the Trinity incarnate, unto Whom with the Father and the Spirit we all sing: Let all creation praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Then we sing the Canticle of the Theotokos:* "My soul doth magnify the Lord," *with the refrain:* "More honorable than the Cherubim."[^1465]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^1466] The Son of the unoriginate Begetter, God and the Lord, incarnate of the Virgin, has appeared unto us to enlighten the darkened, to gather together the scattered; therefore we magnify the all-hymned Theotokos.

*Troparion:* Planted as in Paradise upon the Place of a Skull, O Savior, the most rich tree of Thy most pure Cross, watered by divine blood and water as from a divine fountain from Thy side, O Christ, has budded forth life unto us.

*Troparion:* Thou didst cast down the mighty, being crucified, O All-powerful One; and having raised up human nature lying below in the stronghold of hades, Thou didst seat it upon the Father's throne; with Which when Thou comest, we worshiping magnify Thee.

*Triadikon:* Singing the Unity in three persons, the consubstantial Trinity in orthodox fashion, we faithful glorify: indivisible supremely divine essence, three-sunned never-setting radiance, which has shone forth upon us one imperishable light.

*Another Heirmos:*[^1467] Wholly desire art Thou, wholly sweetness, O Word of God, Son of the Virgin, God of gods, O Lord, the All-holy One of the saints! Therefore we all magnify Thee together with her that bare Thee.

*Troparion:* The knees of the heavenly ones bow unto Thee, O Jesus, as unto the Creator of earthly and nether things, God crucified in the flesh, and every tongue theologizing magnifies Thee.

*Troparion:* Even as Thou didst snatch the bound souls, O Master, from the hands of hades' bonds: so now deliver us from the violent hand of the tyrant, that being delivered, we all may magnify Thee.

*Another Heirmos the same:*[^1468] A rod of strength was given unto corruptible nature, the Word of God in *On Sunday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1469]

*Another Heirmos the same (continued):* ...Thy womb, O Pure One, and it raised this [nature], which had slipped down unto hades; therefore, O All-pure One, we magnify Thee as the Theotokos.

*Troparion:* Graciously receive as intercessor on our behalf Thy Mother, whom Thou wast well-pleased [to choose], O Master, and all things shall be filled with Thy goodness; that we all may magnify Thee as Benefactor.

*After the Katavasia, the Little Ektenia; then:* "Holy is the Lord our God." *Thereafter the Morning Exapostilarion.*[^1470]

*AT THE PRAISES, Resurrection Stichera, Tone 2.*[^1471]

*Sticheron 1:* Every breath and all creation glorifies Thee, O Lord, because by the Cross Thou didst abolish death, that Thou mightest show unto the peoples Thy Resurrection from the dead, as the only Lover of mankind.

*Sticheron 2:* Let the Jews say how the soldiers lost the King while guarding Him? For why did the stone not preserve the Rock of life? Either let them give up Him that was buried, or let them worship Him that is risen, saying with us: Glory to the multitude of Thy compassions, O our Savior, glory to Thee![^1472]

*Sticheron 3:* Rejoice, ye peoples, and be glad! The Angel sitting upon the stone of the tomb, he brought us good tidings, saying: Christ is risen from the dead, the Savior of the world, and has filled all things with fragrance; rejoice, ye peoples, and be glad!

*Sticheron 4:* An Angel indeed brought the "Rejoice" unto her that was full of grace before Thy conception, O Lord, and an Angel rolled away the stone of Thy glorious tomb at Thy Resurrection; the one instead of sorrow proclaiming tokens of gladness, the other instead of death preaching unto us the Master, the Giver of life. Therefore we cry unto Thee: O Benefactor of all, Lord, glory to Thee![^1473]

*Other Stichera, Eastern (by Anatolius), Tone 2.*[^1474]

*Sticheron 1:* The women poured out myrrh with tears upon Thy tomb, and their mouths were filled with joy when saying: The Lord is risen!

*Sticheron 2:* Let the nations and peoples praise Christ our God, Who for our sake willingly endured the Cross, and spent three days in hades; and let them worship His Resurrection from the dead, whereby the ends of the whole world have been enlightened.

*Verse:* "Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end."[^1475]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1476]

*Sticheron 3:* Thou wast crucified and buried, O Christ, as Thou didst will; Thou didst overthrow death and rose in glory, as God and Master, granting unto the world eternal life and great mercy.

*Verse:* "I will praise Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart, I will tell of all Thy marvels."[^1477]

*Sticheron 4:* In truth, O ye lawless ones who sealed the stone, ye have made us worthy of greater marvels! The watchmen have understanding: "Today He came forth from the tomb," they said; "say ye: While we were asleep, His disciples came and stole Him." And who steals a dead body, especially even naked? He Himself rose by His own power as God, having left His burial wrappings in the tomb. Come, see, O ye Jews, how He broke not the seals—He that trampled down death, and grants unending life unto mankind, and great mercy!

*Glory: Evangelical Sticheron. Both now: Theotokion:* "Most blessed art Thou, O Theotokos." *Great Doxology. Troparion:* "Having risen from the tomb."[^1478]

*AT THE LITURGY, Beatitudes, Tone 2.*[^1479]

*Troparion 1:* The voice of the thief do we bring unto Thee, and we pray: Remember us, O Savior, in Thy kingdom.

*Troparion 2:* Thy Cross do we bring unto Thee for the remission of transgressions, which Thou didst accept for our sake, O Lover of mankind.

*Troparion 3:* We worship Thy burial and Thy rising, O Master, whereby Thou didst deliver the world from corruption, O Lover of mankind.

*Troparion 4:* By Thy death, O Lord, death has been swallowed up; and by Thy Resurrection, O Savior, Thou hast saved the world.

*Troparion 5:* Thou didst meet the Myrrhbearers, having risen from the tomb, and didst command [them] to tell Thy disciples of Thy rising.[^1480]

*Troparion 6:* They that slept in darkness, having seen Thee, the Light, in the nethermost depths of hades, O Christ, arose.

*Glory:* Let us glorify the Father, let us all worship the Son, and let us faithfully hymn the Holy Spirit.

*Both now: Theotokion:* Rejoice, fiery throne! Rejoice, unwedded Bride! Rejoice, O Virgin who didst bear God unto men!

*On Sunday Evening, Tone 2.*[^1481]

## Monday: The Holy Bodiless Powers

*On Sunday Evening:*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 2.*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "When from the Tree" (Е҆гда̀ ѿ дре́ва):*[^1482]

*Sticheron 1:* Possessing a fountain of tender compassion, and pouring forth abysses of mercies and streams of bounties, O All-good Father, and Son, the Father's Word, and Holy Spirit, uncreated Nature! Receive our supplication and prayer, granting unto all who are in transgressions forgiveness, as a God compassionate and Lover of mankind.

*Sticheron 2:* Thou hast by nature, as God, a womb of bounties, and mercies, and goodness; therefore we pray Thee, O Christ our Savior, and falling down we call, ever crying unto Thee: Grant unto Thy servants the loosening of many transgressions, and the forgiveness of all wherein we all have sinned, as a God compassionate and Lover of mankind.

*Sticheron 3:* Desiring, O Savior, to save all as God, for whose sake having become incarnate, Thou didst appear as man, save us who worship Thy commandments; for Thou camest not, O Lover of mankind, to save the righteous, but to loose by the grace of divine baptism us who are bound in transgressions by many sins, as a God compassionate and Lover of mankind.[^1483]

*Then the Stichera of the Menaion; but if there be no Menaion, we sing:*
*Other Stichera, to the Bodiless Powers.*[^1484]
*Tone and Podoben the same.*

*Sticheron 1:* Thrones, Cherubim, and Seraphim, Dominions, and Powers, and venerable Authorities, Angels, Archangels, and Principalities with them, unto Him Who composed their imperishable nature, sing an unceasing hymn with one accord, teaching all to honor the one Essence in Trinity, co-originate, equal in honor, and co-enthroned.

*Sticheron 2:* The first ranks of the immaterial choirs, receiving the radiances of the Godhead through immediate stations, according to their order impart the divine splendors unto the others; and through the law of love they also bring them with diligence unto us, according to the worthiness of each one's purity of heart.

*Sticheron 3:* Lifting up on high the soul, on high the eye of the heart and the intellectual aspirations, having divine love in our souls, let us ever stretch forward; that, being illuminated by the rays thence proceeding, we may flee the darkness of passions, hoping to stand with the Angels before the awesome throne of the Creator, and to be transformed from light to light.

*On Sunday Evening, Tone 2.*[^1485]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* The multitude of Angels, O Most Pure One, hymn Thy Son with thrice-holy voices, seeing Thee as His fiery throne and living palace, a divine bridge leading [mortals] ever from earth unto Him, crying unto Thee with one accord with the Archangel Gabriel: "Rejoice, Thou full of grace," for Thou gavest birth to the Fountain of joy.[^1486]

*Then:* "O Gladsome Light." *Prokeimenon:* "Behold now..."[^1487]

*AT THE APOSTICHA, Penitential Stichera, Tone 2.*

*Sticheron 1:* I have sinned against Thee, O Christ Savior, like the prodigal son; receive me, O Father, repenting, and have mercy on me, O God.[^1488]

*Refrain [Verse]:* "Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes... even so our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have compassion upon us."[^1489]

*Sticheron 2:* I cry unto Thee, O Christ Savior, with the voice of the publican: Cleanse me as him, and have mercy on me, O God.[^1490]

*Refrain [Verse]:* "Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us... For our soul hath been exceedingly filled with the reproach of them that are at ease, and the contempt of the proud."[^1491]

*Sticheron 3 [Martyrikon]:* Having not loved earthly sweets, the passion-bearers were accounted worthy of heavenly good things, and became fellow-citizens with the Angels. O Lord, by their prayers have mercy and save us.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Rejoice, O Mary Theotokos, indestructible temple, and rather holy, as the Prophet cries: "Holy is Thy temple, wonderful in righteousness!"[^1492]

*Then:* "Now lettest Thou depart;" *Trisagion; after* "Our Father:" *Troparion to the Saint; Glory... Both now... Theotokion according to the Tone of the troparion. Ektenia and Dismissal.*

*AT COMPLINE, Canon of praise to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^1493]
*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:* In the deep of old, the overwhelming power laid low the Pharaoh-like host; and the incarnate Word destroyed all-wicked sin, the supremely glorified Lord; for gloriously has He been glorified.

*Refrain:* Most holy Lady Theotokos, save us.

*Troparion 1:* Taking form from Thee like unto us, the never-setting Sun clearly showed Thee forth as a spiritual and most radiant heaven. Therefore deliver me from the stench of passions, that with love I may hymn Thee, the only Virgin Mother.

*Troparion 2:* The tongs that bare the noetic Coal, and human nature...[^1494]

*On Sunday at Compline, Tone 2.*[^1495]

*Troparion 2 (continued):* ...that cleansed [it]; I pray Thee, O Lady: wash away the filth of my many transgressions, and deliver me by Thy prayers from defiling passions.

*Glory:* Having given birth unto Christ, the Fountain of mercy, O Most Pure One, and opened Eden unto men: open unto me the doors of Thy mercy, O Theotokos, and grant forgiveness of transgressions, O Lady of the world.

*Both now:* Burst asunder the bonds of my transgressions, O Lady, praying unto Thy Son and God; and cast down the sin that torments me, O Ever-virgin, that, being saved, I may ever hymn Thee, O All-immaculate One.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^1496] Having established me upon the rock of faith, Thou hast enlarged my mouth against mine enemies; for my spirit has rejoiced when singing: There is none holy as our God, and none righteous besides Thee, O Lord.

*Troparion 1:* Dispel the gloom and darkness of my passions by the radiances of Thy prayers, and enlighten me, O most pure Lady, who gavest birth to Christ Who shone forth before the morning star, the never-setting Sun from the Sun.[^1497]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy birthgiving, O Most Pure One, Thou didst raise up the fallen and crushed tabernacle of David Thy forefather; therefore raise up also me, Thy servant, O Theotokos, crushed by passions and transgressions.[^1498]

*Glory:* Be Thou a weapon putting to flight the sins and passions of my soul, O Virgin! And show me forth like another paradise, unharmed and guarded by Thee, and bearing the flowers of the Spirit.

*Both now:* Stretching forth the hand of Thy tender compassion, receive me, O Parent of God, and snatch from all condemnation him that flees unto Thee, and falls down before Thee, and calls upon Thy help.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^1499] I sing to Thee; for by report, O Lord, I heard and was afraid; for Thou comest unto me, seeking me who had strayed. Therefore I glorify Thy great condescension toward me, O Plenteous in mercy.

*Troparion 1:* The undefiled and holy leaven of human nature, taking dough from which the Creator fashioned it; wash away from me also the filth of passions, and cleanse the mire of my transgressions.[^1500]

*Troparion 2:* By the healing medicine of Thy prayers, O Lady, and with plaster and oil, heal Thou now, O Pure One, the scabs and sinful wounds of my soul, *On Sunday at Compline, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 2 (continued):* ...as having given birth unto the God-originate remedy of our nature.

*Glory:* Loose the cords of my passions, O Lady, and sunder the chains of my sins with the weapon of thy prayers, as thou gavest birth unto Christ, Who grants deliverance unto those bound in hades.

*Both now:* Heal the passions of my soul which have darkened me most wretchedly; and enlighten my mind, driving far away, O Pure One, the hordes of demons that afflict me and spread nets for me all the days.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Enlightenment of those lying in darkness, Salvation of the despairing, O Christ my Savior, unto Thee do I rise early, O King of peace! Enlighten me with Thy radiance; for besides Thee I know no other God.[^1501]

*Troparion 1:* As thou hast delivered the world from the ancient curse, O Lady, having given birth unto the Blessing of the world, Christ: deliver me from improper thoughts and all sorrow; for thou alone art the rejoicing of the faithful.

*Troparion 2:* The demonic host surrounds the city of my soul, and strives exceedingly to plunder it. O most glorious Lady, City of the King of all, surround me with towering walls by thy prayers, and save me.

*Glory:* A fierce tempest of perversions of the spirits of wickedness troubles my thoughts; come quickly beforehand and calm it, O thou who gavest birth unto the only Helmsman of all creation, Christ the Almighty.

*Both now:* Cause the light of repentance to shine forth upon me, O Mother of God, who am shut up in destructive darkness; stretch forth a hand of help unto me who lie prostrate, and by thy prayers raise me up, O Pure One, to do the statutes of God.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Wallowing in the abyss of sin, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender compassion: lead me up from corruption, O God![^1502]

*Troparion 1:* As thou gavest birth unto eternal Life, O Most Pure One, put death to death by thy prayers when my soul departs from the body.

*Troparion 2:* The man-hating serpent hunts tempting me; bring him to nought, O Lady, as thou gavest birth unto Him Who crushed the heads of dragons.

*Glory:* Guide unto the straight path me who have walked in perverse paths, O thou who didst bring forth the Way of life, Christ our God.

*On Sunday at Compline, Tone 2.*

*Both now:* Heal the passions of my soul, and overcome the perplexity of grief, O Most Pure One, who didst give birth unto God for the salvation of men.

*Then:* "Lord, have mercy," *thrice.* "Glory... Both now..."

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 2, Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Благоутро́бїа сꙋ́щи и҆сто́чникъ):*[^1503]
Being the wellspring of loving-kindness, account us worthy of mercy, O Theotokos! Look upon a people who have sinned; show forth as always thy power; for hoping in thee, we cry aloud unto thee: "Rejoice!" even as once did Gabriel, Chief Commander of the Bodiless Powers.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Unto the golden image the all-wise youths served not, and went of themselves into the flame, and mocked their gods; amid the flame they cried aloud, and the Angel bedewed them: The prayer of your mouth has already been heard.[^1504]

*Troparion 1:* A wave of passions troubles my mind and shakes my soul; but O Virgin, who didst bring forth Him Who by divine command bridles the surging sea: turn me unto the tranquility of dispassion, and still the carnal disturbance.

*Troparion 2:* Knowing thee to be the door of the spiritual East, manifested from heaven, I, thy servant, pray to enter into heaven through thee, O Virgin. Therefore receive me, O Lady, and guide me by the light.

*Glory:* Christ Thy Son, willingly crucified upon the Cross, put to shame the principalities of darkness, and by death destroyed the corruptive power; by Him, O most pure Lady, mortify my passionate carnal mind.

*Both now:* Cease not, O Pure One, to pray unto God, Who was born of thee, for those who flee with faith unto thee and ask deliverance, O only Protectress of the world, Hope and Protection of the despairing, and ever-present Help of those tempest-tossed in bitter misfortunes.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Disregarding the golden image, the thrice-blessed youths, seeing the immutable and living image of God, sang in the midst of the fire: Let all existing creation praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^1505]

*Troparion 1:* Being the God-fashioned Tabernacle of the Holy of Holies, holding within the great High Priest Who showed forth the figure of the ancient tabernacle: show forth my heart, O Virgin, as a holy tabernacle of the Most High and King of all, Christ.

*Troparion 2:* Having borne the divine ark and the tablets of the covenant, the uncircumscribed...

*On Sunday at Compline, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 2 (continued):* ...Christ, O Virgin, the all-pure Word of God, cause Him to be written as upon the tablet of my soul by the finger of God through thy prayers.

*Glory:* From thy womb was hewn out, O all-immaculate Lady, as from a mountain, the precious Stone, and as the only almighty Ruler He shattered the pillars of all delusion; through Him now destroy the forms of the passions of my soul, having crushed the jaws of spiritual enemies.

*Both now:* With living waters, do thou water my heart dried up by the flame of sin, O Virgin Theotokos, ever preserving me in compunction, as with fear I sing: Let all creation praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Thee who didst ineffably give flesh from virginal loins unto God, the Luminary Who shone forth before the sun and came bodily unto us: O blessed and all-pure one, thee, O Theotokos, do we magnify.[^1506]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst cause Christ, the Sun of righteousness, to shine forth, O Pure One, and wast shown forth as a most radiant heaven; through Him dispel the night of my passions by thy prayers, and enlighten my soul, and illumine and make clear my heart.

*Troparion 2:* Judge, O Theotokos, and overcome my fierce adversary, and deliver me from his affliction; for thou gavest birth unto the good and righteous Judge Who in the bosom of the Godhead condemned death.

*Glory:* He Who as God composed the essences of the bodiless hosts was incarnate of thee, O Virgin Maiden; therefore deliver me, thy servant, by thy prayers from the carnal assault of passions and from carnal mindedness.

*Both now:* Thou didst destroy the grief of Eve, having given birth unto the new Adam, Who created the nature of Adam of old. Therefore tear up also the handwriting of my transgressions, freeing me from temptations, afflictions, and passions.

*Then:* "It is truly meet;" *Trisagion; and after* "Our Father:" *troparia and the rest as usual, and Dismissal.*

*ON MONDAY AT MATINS.*[^1507]

*After the first stichology, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 2.*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* As the waves of the sea, mine iniquities have risen up against me; and like a ship in the deep, I alone...

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Sessional Hymn 1 (continued):* ...am submerged by many transgressions; but guide me into the calm haven of repentance, O Lord, and save me.

*Verse:* O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.[^1508]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* I am an unfruitful tree, O Lord, bearing no fruit of repentance at all; and I fear being cut down, and dread that unquenchable fire. Therefore I pray Thee: before that calamity come, convert and save me.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Being the wellspring of loving-kindness, account us worthy of mercy, O Theotokos! Look upon a people who have sinned; show forth as always thy power; for hoping in thee, we cry aloud unto thee: "Rejoice!" even as once did Gabriel, Chief Commander of the Bodiless Powers.

*After the second stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 2.*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* "Have mercy on me," said David; and I cry unto Thee: I have sinned, O Savior! Cleanse my sins through repentance, and have mercy on me.

*Verse:* O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.[^1509]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* "Have mercy on me, O God, have mercy on me," wept David over two sins; but I cry unto Thee over myriads of transgressions; he bedewed his bed with tears, but I have not acquired even a single drop. I am in despair, and I pray: Have mercy on me, O God, according to Thy great mercy.[^1510]

*Verse:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.[^1511]

*Martyrikon:* Having Thee as their vesture, Who dost deck the heaven with clouds, the holy ones endured torments in the world from the lawless, and abolished the idol delusion. By their prayers deliver us also from the invisible enemy, O Savior, and save us.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Thee do we magnify, O Theotokos, crying: Rejoice, O shut gate, through which ancient paradise was opened unto men!

*After the third stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 2.*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Милосе́рдїа сꙋ́щи и҆сто́чникъ):*[^1512]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Sinning in ignorance and knowledge, by night and by day, angering Thee, O Christ, I alone upon earth by mine iniquities; O only Good and Sinless One, convert and save me by the supplications of Thine Angels, Thou Who camest in tender compassion to call sinners unto repentance.

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O most holy choirs of the Bodiless Powers, pray unto the good God and Master: to spare us...

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Sessional Hymn 2 (continued):* ...in the hour of judgment, from demonic malice and the darkening of passions and from every threatening, who flee with love unto your protection.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* What mother was ever heard of as a virgin? Or what virgin was known as a mother? All things concerning thee are most glorious, O Theotokos! Therefore with faith do we magnify thee.

*Canon of compunction, unto our Lord Jesus Christ, and unto His holy Martyrs.*
*Whose acrostic is: "Grant me a cloud of tears, O Word of God." (A composition of Joseph).*[^1513]
*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:* Come, ye people, let us sing a song unto Christ God, Who divided the people whom He led forth from the bondage of Egypt; for He is glorified.[^1514]

*Troparion 1:* Having become incarnate, O Word, and having come not to call the righteous, as Thou didst say, but sinners unto repentance: receive me who have sinned greatly, and save me.

*Troparion 2:* I alone am enslaved by sin; I alone have opened the doors unto the passions. O only Clement One, having converted me, save me by Thy tender compassion.

*Martyrikon 1:* Before Thy judgment-seat, O Lord, the passion-bearers stand wearing crowns, having overcome the insolence of the evil one and been enriched with immortality.

*Martyrikon 2:* Pouring forth streams of healings for us, the passion-bearers ever dry up the flows of the passions of our flesh by the power of the Holy Spirit.

*Theotokion:* O thou who didst give birth unto the Wellspring of dispassion, heal me who am wounded by passions, O Maiden, and snatch me from eternal fire, O only God-favored one.

*Other Canon, unto the holy heavenly bodiless Angels.*
*Whose acrostic is: "I sing praise unto the Angelic choir." (A composition of Theophanes).*[^1515]
*Ode 1, Tone 2. Heirmos:* Traversing an untrodden, unusual path through the sea without wetness, chosen Israel cried aloud: Let us sing unto the Lord, for gloriously has He been glorified![^1516]

*Troparion 1:* As God-bearing embers, kindled by the brilliance of Thine essence, Thou hast shown forth the bodiless choirs, O Christ, who glorify Thee as Almighty.

*Troparion 2:* Having acquired the power of incorruption and the bestowed glory of immortality, O Christ, the Angels are enlightened by their drawing near unto Thee.

*Troparion 3:* The light-bearing Angels have appeared, showing forth by forms that which is immaterial and according to nature, O Christ, sensibly depicting purity...

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Theotokion:* The angelic ranks serve thy childbearing which is beyond nature, rejoicing, O Pure One! For thou gavest birth unto their God and Lord.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Establish us in Thee, O Lord, Who by the Tree didst slay sin, and plant Thy fear in the hearts of us who sing to Thee.[^1517]

*Troparion 1:* Thou Who didst enter the incorrupt womb, O Christ: renew by repentance my soul which has been corrupted by passions, and show it forth filled with everlasting light.

*Troparion 2:* I gave ear to the enemy who angers Thee, and committed every sin, and foolishly angered Thee, O Lover of mankind, the only Long-suffering One.

*Martyrikon 1:* Warded about by the Cross, the invincible warriors and martyrs of the Savior, like walls, bravely in God tore down the strongholds of deceit.

*Martyrikon 2:* Having strengthened your weakness by divine power, O valiant passion-bearers, ye consigned the might of the wrestler unto utter destruction.

*Theotokion:* O Mary, golden censer, drive away the stench of my passions, and establish me who am shaken by the assaults of the deceitful wrestler.

*Other Heirmos:* The bow of the mighty has been broken by Thy dominion, O Christ, and the weak have girded themselves with strength.[^1518]

*Troparion 1:* He Who by nature is immortal God, wisely working by grace, reveals the immortal hosts. *(Twice)*

*Troparion 2:* Now standing before Christ with utmost longing, pray, ye Angels, that we all may be saved.

*Theotokion:* Truly the Maker of the ages received a temporal beginning from thee, O Mother Ever-virgin.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I sing to Thee; for by report, O Lord, I heard and was afraid; for Thou comest unto me, seeking me who had strayed. Therefore I glorify Thy great condescension toward me, O Plenteous in mercy.[^1519]

*Troparion 1:* I fell into the mire of sin and lost the heavenly fair comeliness, O Lord, and I fear torment. Therefore by the beauty of repentance enlighten my humble soul.

*Troparion 2:* By deceitful words the deceitful one stole me away from Thee, and made me...

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 2 (continued):* ...food for himself; O God of all, snatch me from his malice, and call me back by ways of repentance.

*Martyrikon 1:* Being given over to be food for wild beasts, and burned with fire, O martyrs, and flayed, and torn limb from limb: having Christ as your Helper, ye denied Him not. Pray Him earnestly for me, the wretched one.

*Martyrikon 2:* Honoring the Unity in essence, and Trinity in Persons, the uncreated Godhead, and by no means honoring creation, ye are recognized as Christ's martyrs; therefore ye endured all manner of torments.

*Theotokion:* The Lord was willingly incarnate of thy pure blood and united with men, O Most Pure One! And He receives the repentant who of old were submerged in sins, ever inclined by thy supplications, O Pure One.

*Other Heirmos:* I heard, O Lord, Thy glorious dispensation, and I glorified Thine incomprehensible power, O Lover of mankind.[^1520]

*Troparion 1:* The Bodiless Hosts do I bring unto Thee as intercessors; receiving them, O Bountiful One, as Thou art compassionate, deliver me from sins.

*Troparion 2:* Drawing near unto the All-causing Mind, the divine minds, being purified, are enlightened with transcendent understandings.

*Troparion 3:* The divine adornments of the heavenly hierarchy, being adorned by the Holy Spirit, are preserved immutably.

*Theotokion:* Perceiving thee from afar, O Virgin, as bearing God incarnate upon thine arms, Isaiah foretold.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Giver of light and Creator of the ages, O Lord, guide us in the light of Thy commandments; for besides Thee we know no other God.[^1521]

*Troparion 1:* Thou Who didst enlighten the eyes of the blind man: enlighten my blinded soul, and strengthen it to watch unto good deeds, and to hate slothful sleep unto the end.

*Troparion 2:* Heal, O only Savior, my soul truly wounded by the grievous wound of sin, Thou Who of old didst heal the wounds of him who fell among thieves.

*Martyrikon 1:* The assembly of honorable sufferers put to shame the assembly of lawless persecutors, having endured manifold pains, and now with the sacred Angelic assemblies ever rejoices...

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Martyrikon 2:* As stars of many lights, the martyrs, having appeared most radiant, emit rays of endurance and enlighten the souls of the faithful by the Holy Spirit.

*Theotokion:* Anew, as a child, thou hast borne for us the pre-eternal Son, existing twofold in two wills and operations, as man and at the same time God, O All-hymned One.

*Other Heirmos:* The Coal foreshadowed unto Isaiah, the Sun, shone forth from the Virginal womb, bestowing the enlightenment of divine knowledge upon those that had strayed in darkness.[^1522]

*Troparion 1:* Shining near unto the divine Godhead with brightness, the Cherubim and Seraphim, together with the exalted Thrones, in godlike manner enlighten the rest. *(Twice)*

*Troparion 2:* As Giver of all effusion of light, O Word of God, Thou hast brought forth mirrors of radiant form, which receive Thy beam with gladness and unwavering understanding.

*Theotokion:* The sacred Archangel Gabriel, flying down from heaven, announced joy unto thee, loosening the mourning of the forefathers, O pure Bride of God.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Wallowing in the abyss of sin, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender compassion: lead me up from corruption, O God![^1523]

*Troparion 1:* I have fallen down by the malice of the serpent, and lie upon the bed of despair; O Christ, Who didst raise up paralytics by a word, raise me up also.

*Troparion 2:* Blown about by serpentine winds and ever engulfed by the waves of sin: save me, as Thou didst Peter, O Lord, Lover of mankind.

*Martyrikon 1:* Transcending the bounds of nature, ye endured torments beyond nature; therefore ye were accounted worthy of good things beyond understanding, O martyrs.

*Martyrikon 2:* As good, as beautiful, as honorable, ye were united unto the Good-doing and Beautiful One, ever praying for us, O most glorious martyrs.

*Theotokion:* From all generations the Creator chose thee, O All-immaculate One, the beauty of Jacob which He loved, and shining forth from thee He appeared.[^1524]

*Other Heirmos:* Having heard the voice of suppliant words from an afflicted soul, O Master, deliver me from grievous evils; for Thou alone art the Cause of our salvation.[^1525]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 1:* The divine places of Thy glory, O Master Christ, kindled by radiant flashes of light of the Primal Light, abide eternally in radiant form by its beam. *(Twice)*

*Troparion 2:* Strengthened by divine power, the Seraphim, crying aloud with unceasing voices the thrice-holy hymn, lead us to venerate the three-hypostatic Nature.

*Theotokion:* As the Lord swore in truth of old unto David, He fulfilled, coming forth from thy womb; for thou didst bear, O Maiden, Him Who reigns over heaven and earth.[^1526]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Unto the golden image served in the plain of Dura, Thy three youths paid no heed to the godless decree; but cast into the midst of the fire, bedewed they sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^1527]

*Troparion 1:* By perversion of mind and demonic assaults having been filled with every shame, I went far from Thy commandments like the prodigal; but now turning back I cry: I have sinned like him; abhor me not, O Jesus, Who wast incarnate for my sake.
