*Troparion 2:* O God of all, Who of old didst save the Ninevites when they repented of the threat that brings forth death: likewise, O only Lover of mankind, deliver from bitter torments my heart, defiled with exceeding fornication, which now turns back unto Thee.

*Martyrikon 1:* Grievously wounded was the evildoer who desired to wound you, O divinely blessed and godly martyrs, and he remained unhealed; while your wounds have been shown forth as healing for all the faithful who have been wounded by the assaults of the ancient supplanter.

*Martyrikon 2:* Ye feared neither fierce beasts, nor the threatening of torturers, nor the cleaving sword, nor fiery vessels; but as though in alien bodies ye endured all things, O great martyrs! Therefore have ye been crowned.

*Theotokion:* Thy womb became the dwelling-place of the immaterial Light, O all-pure Bride of God, Maiden, Who by the brightness of divine understanding drove away godlessness; singing unto Whom we cry: Blessed is the God of our fathers!

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Other Heirmos:* Orators were the most wise youths shown to be of old; for theologizing from a God-receiving soul, with lips they sang: O supremely transcendent God of our fathers and our God, blessed art Thou.[^1528]

*Troparion 1:* Around the throne of Thine ineffable glory, the heavenly minds, ever keeping festival, chant with unceasing lips: O supremely transcendent God of our fathers and our God, blessed art Thou. *(Twice)*

*Troparion 2:* When the Angelic ranks beheld Thee ascending into the heavens with flesh, they opened the heavenly gates unto Thee, singing: O supremely transcendent God of our fathers and our God, blessed art Thou.

*Theotokion:* Revealing thee as the fulfillment of the Law and the Prophets, O Maiden, Gabriel cried aloud: Behold, thou alone, O All-hymned One, dost give birth unto the supremely transcendent and blessed God of our fathers and our God.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him Who descended into the fiery furnace unto the Hebrew youths, and changed the flame into dew, praise God as the Lord, ye works, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^1529]

*Troparion 1:* I have fallen under the evil one, and have been enslaved by his wiles, and seeing me in great negligence, the deceitful one boasts; snatch me from him, O Bountiful One, Who art the Return of the strayed.

*Troparion 2:* Loose me who am held fast by indissoluble bodily bonds of passions, Thou Who didst loose the indissoluble from eternal bonds, and guide me thither unto saving paths, O Christ, Savior of the world.

*Martyrikon 1:* The authors of passions truly built all malice upon humble me; but O blessed martyrs, who are truly imitators of Christ's Passions, deliver me from His harm.

*Martyrikon 2:* Not bowing the knee unto idols, ye were cast of old into the furnace of wounds even as the youths, and by divine dew appearing ye remained unburned, O all-praised martyrs, singing unto Christ unto the ages.

*Theotokion:* O Theotokos, Virgin, by thy healing supplications make whole my wretched soul, weakened by many assaults of the evil one, that I may glorify thee unto all ages.

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1530]

*Other Heirmos:* Disregarding the golden image, the thrice-blessed youths, beholding the immutable and living image of God, sang in the midst of the fire: Let all creation, brought into being, praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^1531]

*Troparion 1:* As likenesses of Thy goodness hast Thou composed the Angels, O Word, who perform Thy commandments with might, and help all the faithful to cry: Let all creation, brought into being, praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Troparion 2:* With divine virtues hast Thou adorned the heavenly life, the hierarchy of the holy Angels, illuminating them as they cry unto Thee, O Christ: Let all creation, brought into being, praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Troparion 3:* Rejoicing brightly, let us sing the divine hymn of the incorporeal ones unceasingly, and theologizing the Master, let us cry aloud the song with them: Let all creation, brought into being, praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Theotokion:* Uprightness, which wisely directs all things, worthily loved thee, the all-spotless and all-pure Virgin, having ineffably taken up abode in thee, O most holy Theotokos; Whom we hymn in glory: Let all creation, brought into being, praise the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* God the Word of God, Who by ineffable wisdom came to renew Adam, who through eating had grievously fallen into corruption, and Who for our sake was ineffably incarnate of the Holy Virgin, with one mind we the faithful magnify in songs.[^1532]

*Troparion 1:* Behold, the time for labor! Why sleepest thou without mind, O my soul, in deep despondency? Arise, brighten thy lamp with tears! Make haste, the Bridegroom draws nigh unto souls; linger not, lest thou be left outside the divine doors![^1533]

*Troparion 2:* O how fearful is Thy judgment-seat, revealing every deed bare before Angels and men! O how severe the sentence which Thou shalt deliver unto sinners! Deliver me therefrom, O Christ, granting me tears of conversion before the end!

*Martyrikon:* Marked with the divine blood of the Lamb and Shepherd, the glorious, divine sufferers rejoiced when being slaughtered like innocent lambs; and now they truly illuminate in the heavens the whole holy Church of the firstborn.

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Martyrikon (continued):* Appearing like luminaries of solar radiances, with the rays of your sufferings ye enlighten every soul and drive away all darkness of deception, O noble sufferers; therefore ye are worthily called blessed in faith.

*Theotokion:* Spare me, O Lord, spare me, when Thou art about to judge me, and condemn me not into fire, nor rebuke me in Thy wrath: the Virgin who gave birth unto Thee, O Christ, entreats Thee, with the multitude of Angels, and the assembly of martyrs.

*Other Heirmos:* Wholly desire, wholly sweetness art Thou, O Word of God, Son of the Virgin, God of gods, O Lord, most holy of the saints! Therefore with her who gave Thee birth we all magnify Thee.[^1534]

*Troparion 1:* Appearing in white forms, light-like, the Angels declared unto Thy divine disciples Thy second coming, O Christ; with whom we all, theologizing Thee, magnify Thee. *(Twice)*

*Troparion 2:* As the Benefactor of all rational nature, Thou makest a second brightness for Thy first ray, by reason of Thine extreme goodness; therefore we all, giving thanks, magnify Thee.

*Theotokion:* Understanding thy light-formed virginity, O All-pure One, and marveling, the divine Archistrategos brought joy unto thee; therefore we all magnify thee as Theotokos.

*Then: It is truly meet: Litany, Exapostilarion, and the rest as usual.*

*On the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 2.*

*Sticheron 1:* Meditating upon the improper things, the evils wrought by me, I flee unto Thy compassions, imitating the publican, and the weeping harlot, and the prodigal son. Therefore also I fall down before Thee, O Merciful One: before Thou judgest me, spare me, O God, and have mercy on me.

*Verse:* We have been filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord: - - - And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.[^1535]

*Sticheron 2:* Disregard my transgressions, O Lord born of the Virgin, and cleanse my heart, making it a temple for Thy Holy Spirit. Cast me not away from Thy presence, Thou Who hast measureless, great mercy.

*Verse:* And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and establish Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands establish Thou.[^1536]

*Martyrikon:* Having suffered for Christ even unto death, O passion-bearers, *On Monday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Martyrikon (continued):* O martyrs! Ye have your souls in the heavens in the hands of God, and throughout all the world your relics are venerated. Priests and kings bow down, and all we people, rejoicing, customarily cry: Precious before the Lord is the death of His venerable ones!

*Glory, both now: Theotokion:* Upon thee have we set our hope, O Theotokos, let us not fall away from our expectation! Save us from afflictions, O Helper of the perplexed, and scatter the counsels of the adversaries; for thou art our salvation, O Blessed One.

*Then: It is good to give praise: Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion, and litany; then the First Hour, and dismissal.*

*At the Liturgy, The Beatitudes.*
*Tone 2.*

*Makarismos 1:* The voice of the thief do we bring unto Thee, and we pray: Remember us, O Savior, in Thy kingdom!

*Makarismos 2:* Save me, O Lord my God, and make me a partaker of the lot of them that have loved Thee from the soul.

*Makarismos 3:* All the heavenly hosts with fear serve Thee as God; by their prayers save us.

*Martyrikon:* With the armor of faith, O sufferers, ye conquered the enemy hosts, and were translated unto God.

*Glory:* The intellectual ranks, together with the human race, honor the simple Trinity, the Godhead of one essence.

*Both now: Theotokion:* O all-pure Sovereign Lady, who didst bear God without seed, pray Him that we may be saved.

## Tuesday: The Holy Forerunner[^1537]

*On Monday Evening:*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 2.*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "When from the Tree" (Е҆гда̀ ѿ дре́ва):*[^1538]

*Sticheron 1:* I have surpassed all in sin; what repentance shall I learn? If I sigh like the publican, I seem to weigh down the heavens. If I shed tears like the harlot, with my tears I defile the earth. But grant me remission of sins, O God, and have mercy on me.

*Sticheron 2:* Every sin have I committed, I have surpassed all in fornication. If I desire to repent, I have not streams of tears; if lazily and *On Monday Evening, Tone 2.*

*Sticheron 2 (continued):* now I live, I shall be condemned unto torments. But grant me amendment, O only Good God, and have mercy on me.

*Sticheron 3:* Grant me a cloud of tears of divine compunction, O Christ, as Thou art compassionate, that I may weep and wash away the defilements of my transgressions which I have from pleasures, and appear before Thee cleansed, when Thou shalt come from heaven, O Lord, to judge men, as the one righteous Judge.

*Other Stichera, to the Holy John the Forerunner, Tone and Podoben the same.*

*Sticheron 1:* O herald of Christ and Baptist, Angel, Apostle, Martyr, Prophet! Forerunner, lamp, close friend! Seal of the Prophets, most venerable among them that are born, mediator of the old and the new grace, radiant voice unto the Word! By thy holy intercessions direct the supplications made unto the compassionate God.

*Sticheron 2:* "Behold the Lamb of God, Which takes away the sins of the world!" didst thou cry aloud, O blessed Forerunner, when thou sawest Christ walking upon the earth.[^1539] Pray Him therefore to grant remission of sins unto them that honor thee; for thou hast great boldness toward Him in truth, O Baptist, as mediator of the Law and grace.

*Sticheron 3:* Make haste, and deliver me from temptations, O glorious Prophet of the Lord, I pray thee! For all have stirred up against me, the bitter demons warring against me, seeking to snatch the soul of thine unworthy servant like a wretched bird. Abandon me not utterly, but let them rather understand from thy deeds, O All-blessed One, that thou art a refuge unto me.

*Glory, both now: Theotokion:* As by natural kinship and prayerful agreement, breathing with one breath, O Mother of the King of all, and divine Forerunner! Make supplication together for the servant who hath greatly angered the Good and Merciful One, that having inclined unto your intercessions, He may show compassion and save, and deliver me from kinship with the fire.

*Prokeimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord:*

*On the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 2.*

*Sticheron 1:* I have sinned against Thee, O Savior, like the prodigal son; receive me, O Father, as I repent, and have mercy on me, O God.

*Verse:* Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes - - - So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have compassion on us.[^1540]

*Sticheron 2:* I cry unto Thee, O Christ Savior, with the voice of the publican: Cleanse me even as him, and have mercy on me, O God.

*Verse:* Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us: - - - For our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and the contempt of the proud.[^1541]

*On Monday Evening, Tone 2.*

*Martyrikon (continued):* When the holy martyrs pray for us and sing of Christ, all deception ceases, and the human race is saved by faith.

*Glory, both now: Theotokion:* Inpassable gate, mystically sealed, blessed Virgin Theotokos! Receive our supplications, and bear them unto Thy Son and God, that through thee He may save our souls.

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion of the Saint; Glory, both now: Theotokion in the tone of the troparion. Litany and dismissal.*

*At Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 2.*[^1542]

*Ode 1. Heirmos:* The overdignified power of old cast down in the deep Pharaoh's entire army; and the incarnate Word destroyed all-wicked sin, the most glorious Lord; for gloriously hath He been glorified.[^1543]

*Refrain:* Most holy Sovereign Lady Theotokos, save us.

*Troparion 1:* An ever-blooming crown of praise, which the Church hath budded forth by the Spirit, O Pure One, she hymnographically brings unto thee, the Bride of God, together with the Archangel Gabriel, singing: Rejoice! and honorably crowning thee.

*Troparion 2:* Having cultivated the Life-bearing Vine, Christ God, O all-hymned Mother of God, thou hast been revealed beyond understanding as the holy land of the Father, O full of God's grace, and hast given drink unto the whole world with life-bearing drink, O Pure One.

*Glory:* Amid the whole world which is grievously tossed by waves and overwhelmed with transgressions, thou, O All-pure One, didst give birth unto the Helmsman, Christ, both the God and Lord of us all, Who faithfully guides us unto the saving haven.

*Both now: Theotokion:* O refuge and city of salvation, we all faithfully pray thee, O Mary Mother of God, and warmly make entreaty: Receive our supplications, of thy faithful servants, and loose us all from the condemnation of transgressions.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Establish us in Thee, O Lord, Who by the Tree didst put sin to death, and plant Thy fear in the hearts of us who sing of Thee.[^1544]

*Troparion 1:* The deception of Eve became the beginning of God's turning away from men; but the holy Theotokos hath brought God back again unto us.

*Troparion 2:* The glorious Joachim gives birth unto thee for the world, O All-hymned One, as the treasure of life, and rejoices in goodly offspring on thy account, as hope that came unto childless parents.

*On Monday Evening at Compline, Tone 2.*

*Glory:* From Anna joy blossomed forth unto the human race; for thou as a Virgin dost give birth unto the King, and women rejoice together with thy birth-giving, having been loosed from the curse through thee.

*Both now: Theotokion:* The bush on Sinai prefigured thy most glorious birth-giving, O Virgin; for it was not burned by the fire of the Godhead, when receiving it in thy womb, O pure one.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I heard, O Lord, of Thy glorious dispensation, and I glorified Thine incomprehensible power, O Lover of mankind.[^1545]

*Troparion 1:* Jacob divinely foresaw thee as a ladder, O Virgin, upon the top whereof God was established.[^1546]

*Troparion 2:* The descent of Angels, which was the coming of the Word unto us, was shown forth through thee, O All-pure One.

*Glory:* Blessed is thy womb, O All-hymned One, and blessed are the breasts, O Virgin; for on their account we all found life.

*Both now: Theotokion:* Mystically do we sing of thee, O Mother of God, with Orthodox voices; for the Church is preserved by thine intercessions.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* The Enlightenment of them that lie in darkness, the Salvation of the despairing, O Christ my Savior! Unto Thee do I rise early, O King of peace; enlighten me with Thy radiance, for other than Thee I know no God.[^1547]

*Troparion 1:* More honorable than the Ark of the Law do we hymn thee, O Theotokos Mary! For as tablets hast thou borne the Creator of all and God, O All-hymned One.

*Troparion 2:* As the throne of God the Word do we glorify thee, O Theotokos, upon which God, sitting as man, appeared, and thou becamest higher than the Cherubim.

*Glory:* Thou hast loosed the whole human race from bitter bondage, O Virgin, and by the freedom of Christ hast thou honored woman's nature in thy divine childbearing.

*Both now: Theotokion:* As a Virgin thou didst give birth unto the Son, and women manifestly conquer the enemy; therefore they flee unto thee, O Maiden, keeping their virginity.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Rolling in the abyss of sin, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy loving-kindness: From corruption, O God, lead me up![^1548]

*Troparion 1:* O pure Theotokos, who art joy unto the world, Rejoice! do we faithfully cry unto thee with the Angel: Count us also worthy of thy joy, and destroy our grief.

*On Monday Evening at Compline, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 2:* O dwelling-place of unassailable joy, count them worthy of thy joy who faithfully praise thee, O Mother Ever-Virgin, and destroy our grief.

*Glory:* Higher than the heaven of heavens hast thou been revealed, O Bride of God, by thy divine glory; for our God, having taken up abode in thee, appeared unto me.

*Both now: Theotokion:* Now hath woman's nature rejoiced, now hath sorrow ceased, joy hath blossomed forth; for Mary hath given birth unto Joy, the Savior, Christ God.

*Then: Lord, have mercy, thrice. Glory, both now:*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 2.*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Милосе́рдїа сꙋ́щи):*[^1549]

*Sessional Hymn:* Beholding the grievous sluggishness of my soul and the faintness of my heart, O Mother of God, heal them by thine intercessions, and make me worthy of the lot of the saved, delivering me from darkness and torment, as thou art my only hope and consolation.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* When the golden image was worshiped in the plain of Dura, Thy three youths disregarded the godless decree; and cast into the midst of the fire, bedewed, they sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^1550]

*Troparion 1:* Gideon's fleece of old prefigured the descent of God the Word upon thee, O Pure One; for like dew didst thou receive the conception, O incorrupt Virgin.[^1551] Therefore we all cry unto thee: Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Pure One!

*Troparion 2:* New and fearful, faithful and marvelous are thy mysteries, O Mary, Mother of Christ our God, for through thee we have all been reconciled unto God and the Master; and with the Angels we now sing: Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Pure One!

*Glory:* Gideon of old manifestly heralded beforehand thy divine childbearing, O Pure One, bringing a bowl filled with water wrung from the fleece; for He wholly and divinely took up abode in thee, O All-pure One: Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Pure One!

*Both now: Theotokion:* Having given birth unto God and the Savior of all, O Mary, thou hast been the correction of the despairing, the renewal of sinners, the hope of the hopeless, and the help of them that sing: Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Pure One!

*On Monday Evening at Compline, Tone 2.*

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him Who descended into the fiery furnace unto the Hebrew youths, and changed the flame into dew, praise God as the Lord, ye works, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^1552]

*Troparion 1:* A new paradise hast thou been revealed unto us, O most holy Theotokos, who without seed hast sprouted forth not a tree of death, but of life, as an orchard, even the Lord, by Whom we are all nourished with immortal life.

*Troparion 2:* The whole Church of Christ sings of thy birth-giving, O Theotokos, for all the sinners and the poor who flee unto thee with love are saved; for Christ came upon earth to save men.

*Glory:* The foremother is loosed from condemnation through thee, O Virgin Theotokos! And behold, now women suffer for Christ, and woman's nature rejoices, even as the protomartyr Thecla cries aloud.[^1553]

*Both now: Theotokion:* None hath perished, O Pure One, who with Orthodox faith hath acquired hope toward thee, O Virgin Mother of God, save only he who through envy refuses to bow down before thine icon as depicted.[^1554]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The Luminary God Who shone forth before the sun, Who came bodily unto us from a virginal flank, and was ineffably incarnate, O blessed all-pure one, thee, O Theotokos, do we magnify.[^1555]

*Troparion 1:* Incline thine ear unto me, O most holy Virgin, as with laudatory words I faithfully sing of thy birth-giving; and having received the song of my lips like the widow's gifts, ask forgiveness of my sins.

*Troparion 2:* Thy beauty shines, the splendor of purity gleams, O Pure One, and more than these thy childbearing outshines; for God, the Creator of the sun and of all creation, was born of thee. Therefore we all magnify thee.

*Glory:* Hymning thee divinely in songs as the light of purity, the rod of virginity, and Mother of God, O Theotokos, with a voice of praise we pray thee: Establish us in virginity, and preserve us in purity!

*Both now: Theotokion:* Having kept body and soul undefiled for God, O Pure One, Christ the King desired thy beauty, and showed thee forth as the Mother of His Incarnation, O most glorious Mary, working out my salvation.

*Then: It is truly meet: and a prostration. Trisagion, and after Our Father: troparia and the rest as usual; and dismissal.*

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1556]

*After the first Stichology, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 2.*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Like the billows of the sea have my transgressions risen up against me; and like a ship in the deep, I alone am submerged by many sins; but into the calm haven of repentance guide me, O Lord, and save me.

*Verse:* O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, neither chasten me in Thy wrath.[^1557]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* A barren tree am I, O Lord, producing no fruit of repentance at all; and I fear felling, and am terrified of that unquenchable fire. Therefore I pray Thee: Before that disaster, turn and save me.

*Glory, both now: Theotokion:* Partakers of the divine nature have we become through thee, O Virgin Theotokos! For thou didst give birth unto God incarnate for us. Therefore, as is our debt, we all piously magnify thee.

*After the second Stichology, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 2.*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Have mercy on me, said David; and I cry unto Thee: I have sinned, O Savior! Cleanse my sins by repentance, and have mercy on me.

*Verse:* O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, neither chasten me in Thy wrath.[^1558]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Have mercy on me, O God, have mercy on me! Over two sins David wept, while I cry unto Thee over myriads of transgressions; he bedewed his couch with tears, while I have not acquired even a single drop.[^1559] I despair, and I pray: Have mercy on me, O God, according to Thy great mercy!

*Verse:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.[^1560]

*Martyrikon:* Thou Who didst enlighten Thy saints more than gold, and didst glorify Thy venerable ones, as Thou art good: Being implored by them, O Christ God, direct our life, as Lover of mankind, and set our prayer right like incense, O Thou Who alone restest in the saints.

*Glory, both now: Theotokion:* O Theotokos, despise me not who require the protection which is from thee; for upon thee hath my soul set its hope: have mercy on me.

*After the third Stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 2.*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Благоꙋтро́бїа сꙋ́щи):*[^1561]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* By reason of the mercy of Thy loving-kindness, O Good One, Thou camest to save Thy creation, O Christ, having bowed the heavens by Thy descent;

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Sessional Hymn 1 (continued):* therefore, singing of Thine awesome dispensation, we cry unto Thee: By the supplications of Thy Forerunner, grant us cleansing from sins, as Thou alone art compassionate!

*Sessional Hymn 2:* In the streams of the Jordan didst thou immerse the unstinting wellspring of loving-kindness, O John; therefore I diligently pray thee: By thine acceptable prayers, guide unto the haven of life me who am daily and grievously overwhelmed by many passions in the deep of worldly life.

*Glory, both now: Theotokion:* Who hath seen, who hath heard of a Mother giving birth without knowing a man unto her Creator, suckling Him Who gives food unto all flesh! O wonder! Thy womb hath been revealed as the Cherubic throne, O Theotokos full of grace: pray for our souls.

*Canon of Compunction to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to the Holy Martyrs.*[^1562]
*(Whose acrostic is this: Unto my lamentations Thou grantest room, O Savior. A composition of Joseph).*
*Tone 2. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Having taken up the Song of Moses, cry aloud, O my soul: A helper and a protector is He become unto me for salvation; this is my God, and I will glorify Him![^1563]

*Troparion 1:* Let us bitterly weep for ourselves, brethren, before our departure, that by good tears we may then escape the tears of torment, which possess nothing profitable.

*Troparion 2:* Ten thousand times have I promised to repent, O Christ, yet having an unfeeling soul, I fall into transgressions; have compassion, O Savior, upon my weakness!

*Martyrikon 1:* Ye who through divine dew endured the fire of torture, O passion-bearers of Christ: Deliver me from the fire of Gehenna, who lie bedridden in grievous passions!

*Martyrikon 2:* Having shown yourselves mighty against the enemies by divine power, ye cast down their powerless strength, O praiseworthy martyrs of Christ.

*Theotokion:* O fire-bearing tongs, which Isaiah saw of old, burn up the material passions of my heart, O Parent of God, and utterly consume them![^1564]

*Another Canon, to the Holy and Most Honorable Prophet John the Forerunner.*[^1565]
*(Whose acrostic is this: O Baptist, receive my supplication. A composition of Joseph).*
*Tone 2. Ode 1. Heirmos:* The overdignified power of old cast down in the deep Pharaoh's entire army; and the incarnate Word destroyed all-wicked sin, the most glorious Lord; for gloriously hath He been glorified.[^1566]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*[^1567]

*Troparion 1:* O Baptist and Forerunner of Christ, direct my mind which is ever submerged by carnal pleasures, and calm the waves of passions, that being in divine stillness, I may hymn thee in song.

*Troparion 2:* Having been enlightened with unsearchable illumination, like a most luminous star, thou didst run before the spiritual Orient; pray Him, O Baptist, that my heart may be illumined, which is darkened by all the assaults of demons.

*Troparion 3:* In the river of old, O all-wise one, thou didst immerse the Abyss, Who by grace drowns the deluge of all transgression. But I pray thee, O Blessed One: dry up the torrents of my transgressions by thy divine intercession.

*Theotokion:* Thou wast the kinsman of the pure Virgin who gave birth unto God in the flesh, O blessed Forerunner! With her we honor and entreat thee: Do thou make us, who now dwell in thy divine temple, temples of the Holy Spirit.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Show my barren mind to be fruitful, O God, Cultivator of good things, Planter of blessings, through Thy tender-heartedness.[^1568]

*Troparion 1:* With sluggish slumber have I weighed down my soul; raise me up, O Christ, unto the vigil of repentance, to do Thy commandments.

*Troparion 2:* Show me not despairing, O Jesus, in the fearful day; but having turned me before the end, deliver me from grievous torment.

*Martyrikon 1:* Ye who nobly imitated the sufferings of Christ, passion-bearers of Christ, divine examples: heal the grievous passions of my soul.

*Martyrikon 2:* That ye might be vouchsafed the everlasting good things in the heavens, O passion-bearers, ye steadfastly endured every trial of afflictions upon the earth.

*Theotokion:* Thou sucklest in motherly fashion the Nourisher of all, O Pure One, and holdest Him in thine embrace Who ever upholds all things with His hand.

*Other Heirmos:* Having established me upon the rock of faith, Thou hast enlarged my mouth against mine enemies; for my spirit rejoiced when singing: There is none holy as our God, and none is righteous beside Thee, O Lord.[^1569]

*Troparion 1:* Heal the bruises of my soul, and my mind darkened by neg- *On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 1 (continued):* -ligence, illumine by thy divine mediation, O Forerunner of the Lord, and deliver me from every adverse circumstance, I pray thee.

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst loose the barrenness of her who bare thee, having been born by the providence of God, O all-wise Prophet; make now therefore my barren heart fruitful by thine intercession, O Forerunner of the Lord, to bring forth the sprouts of virtues.

*Troparion 3:* Entreat that he who with love for thee builds a divine house may attain unto the life on high; and make them that serve thy church in faith temples of the divine Spirit, O Baptist and Forerunner, by thine intercessions.

*Theotokion:* The Forerunner leaped with joy in his mother's womb, and worshipped the Lord carried in Thy womb, O Full of grace; entreat Him to deliver me from every sorrow.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Foreseeing Thy birth from the Virgin, the Prophet proclaimed aloud, crying: I have heard Thy report, and was afraid; for from Teman, from the holy and overshadowing mountain, art Thou come, O Christ![^1570]

*Troparion 1:* Seeing me robbed on every side and impoverished, O Word, the crafty schemer, the enemy, rejoices over my perdition. O Lord of glory, Enricher of the poor, rescue me from his villainy.

*Troparion 2:* I have defiled my hands and eyes, O Lord, having committed what is improper to do, and have moved Thy compassions unto anger, exhausting Thy longsuffering; look down and take pity upon me, O Good One.

*Martyrikon 1:* How marvelous is our God in His saints, who served Him, casting down the idols of destruction, and inheriting the spacious expanse of Paradise, whence Adam was driven of old.

*Martyrikon 2:* By the streams of your blood, O blessed ones, ye brought to an end the blood once offered unto demons, destructive unto all, which men in deception offered unto their own ruin. Therefore ye are ever called blessed.

*Theotokion:* Instructed by the Spirit, the glorious choir of the Prophets, in diverse forms prefigured this mystery of thine, O Theotokos, which transcends the mind, in sa- *On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Theotokion (continued):* -cred figures whose fulfillment we now brightly behold.

*Other Heirmos:* Thou didst come from the Virgin, not an ambassador, nor an angel, but Thyself incarnate, O Lord, and hast saved all of me, a man; therefore I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^1571]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst bend the head of Him Who bowed the heavens and conversed with men, with thy right hand, O most wealthy one! Preserving my heart in humility, preserve me thereby.

*Troparion 2:* The untrodden wilderness had thee as a citizen, O blessed Forerunner; therefore I cry unto thee: Make fruitful my soul, which hath become barren of every divine deed.

*Troparion 3:* Guiding the divine law, thou wast unlawfully slain; therefore I pray thee: Correct me, who am ever transgressing and led astray by demonic deceits.

*Troparion 4:* Having built thyself as a temple unto the Master and King, thou hast now departed unto the divine habitations, O Forerunner; entreat that he who erected a most holy house unto thee may attain unto them.

*Theotokion:* Look down upon me who am diseased, O All-immaculate One, and loose my grievous and hard-to-heal passions, that I may magnify thee who hast magnified all mankind.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Having dispersed the mist of my soul, O my Savior, illumine me with the light of Thy commandments, as Thou alone art the King of peace.[^1572]

*Troparion 1:* Senselessly I join sins unto sins, and there is no lifting up of the head in my death. Woe is me! How shall I appear before Christ?

*Troparion 2:* Storm-tossed like a ship, I have lost my time which Thou gavest me, O Compassionate One; and now, impoverished, I cry: Despise me not, O Christ.

*Martyrikon 1:* As ye despised earthly and trampled glory, O passion-bearers, ye were vouchsafed heavenly glory, co-abiding with Christ.

*Martyrikon 2:* Having estranged the mind from the love of the flesh by faith, ye gladly endured torments, O passion-bearers, being claimed as Christ's own.

*Theotokion:* Daniel beheld thee in the Spirit as a great mountain, O Theotokos, from which a stone was cut without hands, which crushes the idols of the demons.[^1573]

*Other Heirmos:* Illumination of them that lie in darkness, salvation of the despairing, O Christ my Savior, unto *On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Other Heirmos (continued):* Thee do I rise early, O King of peace! Illumine me with Thy brightness; for beside Thee I know no other God.[^1574]

*Troparion 1:* Having baptized in the streams of the Jordan Christ, the torrent of incorruption, O Forerunner, pray Him to dry up the outpourings of my passions, and that I may inherit the torrents of delight and the fair rejoicings of the righteous.

*Troparion 2:* Already I weep and shudder with fear, and am ever gripped with perplexity, when contemplating my deeds and the fearful future judgment. O tender-hearted Lord, spare me by the entreaties of Thy Baptist.

*Troparion 3:* Prescribing salvation for the people, O Forerunner, which came to pass in the repentance of transgressions, thou didst stand between the Law and grace; therefore we pray thee: Enlighten us by the patterns of repentance.

*Troparion 4:* Grant me time for repentance, O Benefactor, who have despondently spent and wasted all the past, having to entreat Thee for this, O Word, John the great Forerunner and universal preacher of repentance.

*Theotokion:* By the plots and snares of the crafty one have I been slain; O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady, revive me, who didst bear the hypostatic Life of all, O Mother of God, that I may piously sing of thee, the all-immaculate.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* In the deep of sin am I held, O Savior, and in the tempest of life am I storm-tossed; but as Thou didst bring up Jonah from the monster, so also lead me up from passions, and save me.[^1575]

*Troparion 1:* Like the Canaanite woman of old, I cry unto Thee, O Savior: Son of God, having had mercy, take pity on me; for I have a soul suffering in evils, and now unwilling of itself to come to its senses.[^1576]

*Troparion 2:* The tempest of immeasurable passions troubles me; even as of old Thou didst rebuke the sea and save Thy holy Disciples, so also lead me up, O Jesus, and save me, O Christ.

*Martyrikon 1:* The bodiless angelic ranks marveled at your endurance in the flesh, and praised Him Who grants unto you, O honorable sufferers, both strength and the reward of labors.

*Martyrikon 2:* Sprinkled with the streams of your own blood, pierced in the eyes, O martyrs, and frozen with bitter cold, ye passed over unto the warmth of life, singing of Christ.

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Theotokion:* As a table thou holdest the mystical Bread, whereof eating we hunger no more, O All-hymned One, knowing thee truly as the Parent and Nourisher of Christ, the God of all.

*Other Heirmos:* Wallowing in the abyss of sin, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy loving-kindness: Lead me up from corruption, O God![^1577]

*Troparion 1:* O voice that didst preach the Word, receiving the voices of all, pray that forgiveness of sins may be granted unto them that sing of thee in faith.

*Troparion 2:* Heal the brokenness of my soul, loose the burden of sins, and beyond hope save me by thy prayers, O blessed Forerunner.

*Troparion 3:* Pray Jesus, Whom thou didst baptize with thy hand, O Forerunner, to deliver from the hand of sin me who ever lift up my hands unto Him, O All-glorious One.

*Theotokion:* By sluggish slumber am I held fast; the sleep of sin weighs down my heart; by thy vigilant intercession, O Most Pure One, raise me up and save me.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Imitating the Cherubim, the youths danced in the furnace, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God, for in truth and judgment hast Thou brought all these things upon us by reason of our sins, O supremely praised and supremely glorified unto all ages![^1578]

*Troparion 1:* I have rejected Thy laws and submitted unto irrational lusts, committing improper deeds, O Christ; for I was exposed in my folly as no other among men upon earth. Leave me not therefore to perish, O Savior, for Thy love of mankind.

*Troparion 2:* In iniquities, O Lord, behold, was I conceived, as David cries, and like a provoking servant I tremble; for I have provoked Thee, the only good God. Leave me not therefore to perish, O Savior, for Thy love of mankind.[^1579]

*Martyrikon 1:* Manfully striving, the assembly of passion-bearers was martyrized and crowned by the life-bearing right hand; for truly they loved God, Who made all things by His word. And now, rejoicing in the heavens, they delight in the divine inheritance.

*Martyrikon 2:* Pierced in the eyes, and shorn of hands and feet, nobly *On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Martyrikon 2 (continued):* the glorious ones hastened on their course toward the heavenly journey, thwarting the marches of the solitary champion. By their prayers save all, O Word, who sing Thy praise.

*Theotokion:* Cherubim glorify, Seraphim hymn, Thrones, Dominions, and Powers ever praise thy birthgiving which is beyond understanding, O all-hymned Mary, for thou alone gavest birth unto God in the flesh; entreat Him, O Pure One, that we all may be saved who honor thee with love.

*Other Heirmos:* The god-opposing decree of the lawless tyrant raised a high flame; but Christ spread spiritual dew over the pious youths: He Who is blessed and supremely glorified.[^1580]

*Troparion 1:* Rooting out from the root the wounds of my passionate heart with the axe of thy repentance, plant therein, O Forerunner, divine dispassion and the purest fear of God, which estranges me from all malice.

*Troparion 2:* Since thou didst baptize in the streams of the Jordan the Lord Who covers His chambers with the waters, pray Him, O glorious Forerunner, ever to grant the water of divine compunction unto mine eyes.

*Troparion 3:* O Forerunner, who didst preach the Lamb of God Who takes away the sin of the world, pray Him to show me a stranger to the portion of the goats, and to unite me also, O Glorious One, unto His sheep on the right hand.

*Theotokion:* A barren womb bare thee, O Virgin, who didst bear the incarnate Word in thy belly; Whom the great Forerunner, the all-holy Fruit of a barren mother, recognized with divine leapings, rejoicing, and worshipped.

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him Who of old in the bush on Mount Sinai prefigured unto Moses the wonder of the Virgin, sing, bless, and supremely exalt unto all ages![^1581]

*Troparion 1:* That Thou mightest deify us, Thou wast incarnate out of mercy; but I in no wise understood this, being enslaved by pleasures. In Thy goodness turn me, O Christ, the Salvation of all.

*Troparion 2:* Thou art the Good Shepherd, O Word; my wretched soul that hath wandered upon the mountains of transgression turn and save, lest the crafty enemy utterly devour me.

*Martyrikon 1:* Let us manfully stand together, cried the fair sufferers one to another, being grievously wounded:

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Martyrikon 1 (continued):* Behold, Christ stretches forth the crowns of victory unto all ages!

*Martyrikon 2:* With strong sinews, by your steadfast pains, ye choked the serpent who maliciously desired to seduce you; and ye were shown to be heirs of the delights of Paradise.

*Theotokion:* That God might deify us, He was incarnate of thy pure blood, and became man, O Virgin Theotokos! Him ever entreat for them that honor thee.

*Other Heirmos:* The fiery furnace in Babylon of old divided its operations by the decree of God, consuming the Chaldeans, but bedewing the faithful, who sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^1582]

*Troparion 1:* Give a right hand unto me who lie upon the earth, O Forerunner, who didst stretch forth thy right hand and wash with waters the Spotless One; and deliver me from carnal defilement, cleansing all of me by repentance, and save me.

*Troparion 2:* Having time to repent, O my soul, shake off the heavy sleep of sloth, and watch diligently, crying aloud unto thy Master: O Compassionate One, take pity on me by the entreaties of Thy Baptist!

*Troparion 3:* Torrents of passions and waters of wickedness have entered into my soul, O blessed Forerunner! Make haste quickly to rescue me, who with river streams didst wash the calmest deep of dispassion.

*Troparion 4:* Woe is me, who have committed many evils! Woe is me, who alone have provoked the all-good God! O Baptist of Christ, help me, and grant remission of my transgressions and the cutting away of my debts, through thine intercessions.

*Theotokion:* Having given birth in the flesh unto the Most High God, raise me up from the mire of passions which afflict me; and me who am grievously and wholly impoverished, enrich with divine virtues, O Most Pure One, that being saved, I may sing of thee.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Who among the earthborn hath heard of such a thing, or who hath ever seen, that a virgin was found with child in the womb, and without travail bare an infant? Such is thy wonder, and thee, O pure Mother of God, do we magnify.[^1583][^1584]

*Troparion 1:* O how fearful is Thy judgment-seat, whereon I expect to be judged, O Christ, yet feel not at all this fear, passing all my years in sloth- *On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 1 (continued):* -fulness! Turn me, O only Creator, Who didst turn Manasseh who had sinned.[^1585]

*Troparion 2:* Stay the torrents of my measureless evils, O Christ, I cry unto Thee; give me streams of tears that wash away the defilements which I have incurred through folly; and save me, Who didst save the harlot who repented from the soul, by Thy mercy.

*Martyrikon 1:* The radiant memory of the divine passion-bearers, having shone upon us like the sun, guides with light all the ends of the earth, and by the divine Spirit drives away the darkness of idolatrous frenzy and the gloom of soul-corrupting passions.

*Martyrikon 2:* An honorable regiment, a victorious army, and a chosen host, the assembly of holy martyrs, a blessed choir, was joined in dance with the Bodiless hosts. By their prayers, O Christ, make us all partakers of Thy kingdom.

*Theotokion:* With the bright rays of Him Who shone forth unto us from thy womb and abolished the night of godlessness, O Virgin Mother Mary, enlighten all who honor thee in faith, and deliver them from unillumined darkness in the hour of judgment.

*Other Heirmos:* The Son of the unoriginate Begetter, God and Lord, incarnate of the Virgin, hath appeared unto us, to enlighten them that are darkened, to gather the dispersed; therefore the all-hymned Theotokos do we magnify.[^1586]

*Troparion 1:* Deliver me from sinful mire, O Lord, Who alone art sinless and abounding in mercy, by the entreaties of the Baptist, who proclaimed Thee unto all the world as the Lamb of God, Which takes away the sins of men.

*Troparion 2:* Having thee as a fragrant rose, as a sweet-smelling cypress, as an unfading lily, as precious myrrh, O Forerunner of the Lord, I am delivered from the foul stench of my deeds by thy prayers, fleeing unto thy protection.

*Troparion 3:* Make me, O all-blessed one, to be unbarren of unfruitful deeds, ever bringing forth the fair fruit of virtues, making me a child of the Lord, and a partaker of the divine kingdom, and a fellow citizen with the choir of the saints.

*Troparion 4:* Unto us who love thee and honor thee with love, and celebrate in thy divine church, grant *On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 4 (continued):* from heaven deliverance from grievous things, O Forerunner of the Lord, and amendment of life, and deliverance from transgressions.

*Theotokion:* Carried in the womb of the Mother of God, thou didst worship Him Who carries all things by His nod, O Prophet. With her entreat Him, that my humble soul may be saved, which falls into many transgressions all the days.

*Then: It is truly meet: and a prostration, litany, Exapostilarion, and the usual psalms.*

*On the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 2.*

*Sticheron 1:* Meditating upon the improper things, the evils wrought by me, I flee unto Thy compassions, imitating the publican, and the weeping harlot, and the prodigal son. Therefore also I fall down before Thee, O Merciful One: before Thou judgest me, spare me, O my God, and have mercy on me.

*Verse:* We have been filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord: - - - And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.[^1587]

*Sticheron 2:* Disregard my transgressions, O Lord born of the Virgin, and cleanse my heart, making it a temple for Thy Holy Spirit; cast me not away from Thy presence, Thou Who hast measureless, great mercy.

*Verse:* And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and establish Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands establish Thou.[^1588]

*Martyrikon:* Having taken up the Cross of Christ, the holy martyrs, an invincible weapon, abolished all the power of the devil; and having received heavenly crowns, they became a wall unto us, praying unto Him for us.

*Glory, both now: Theotokion:* Save Thy servants from perils, O Virgin Theotokos, for after God we all flee unto thee, as unto an unbreakable wall and protectress.

*Then: It is good to give praise: Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion and litany; then the First Hour.*

*At the Liturgy, The Beatitudes, Tone 2.*

*Makarismos 1:* The voice of the thief do we bring unto Thee, and we cry aloud unto Thee: Remember us, O Lord, in Thy kingdom![^1589]

*Makarismos 2:* I have surpassed the thief and the harlot in passions; take pity on me, O Savior, who am self-condemned.

*Makarismos 3:* O Forerunner, who didst immerse the abyss of loving-kindness in the water, by *On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Makarismos 3 (continued):* thy prayers diminish my passions.

*Martyrikon:* Having dried up the deceitful streams by the streams of your blood, O passion-bearers of Christ, ye are worthily glorified.

*Glory:* Unto the unoriginate Trinity do I bow down: unto the Father, and the Son, and the upright Spirit; by nature I know Thee as a simple and three-hypostatic Unity.

*Both now: Theotokion:* The Fruit of thy womb, O Pure One, hath again made dwellers of Paradise them that slipped through the fruit.

## Wednesday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^1590]

*On Tuesday Evening.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 2.*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "When from the Tree" (Е҆гда̀ ѿ дре́ва):*[^1591]

*Sticheron 1:* Unable to bear the sight, the luminaries were darkened when Thou, O Word, didst set upon the Cross! The earth was shaken, and the rocks were split, the adornment of the temple was rent in twain, the graves were opened, the dead arose, Hades yielded up all them that were below, the demons were conquered, and death was accounted a sleep unto all.[^1592]

*Sticheron 2:* When the well-disposed thief beheld Thee, O Christ, the fruitful Vine, He became the best of thieves, well skilled, and stole forgiveness of his ancient transgressions, very naturally with a small voice. Let us hasten to be emulators of him, all crying aloud: Remember us also, O Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 3:* Truly Thy divine Cross, O Christ, shines like a star in heaven within the Church, consuming demons, but illuminating the faithful, and putting to shame the faces of them that crucified Thee; whose forefathers Thou didst lead forth of old from servitude by the tree which prefigured the Cross, and in the wilderness madest them suck honey from the rock.[^1593]

*Then the Stichera of the Menaion; but if there be no Menaion, we sing:*

*On Tuesday Evening.*
*Other Stichera to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "When from the Tree" (Е҆гда̀ ѿ дре́ва):*[^1594]

*Sticheron 1:* As mediatress unto all men, as a great refuge unto all, as a protectress unto all Christians did He show thee forth, O Lady—He who from thee in His tender compassion clothed Himself in our mortal nature,[^1595] and endured crucifixion and death for our sake. Him therefore beseech unceasingly, O Bride of God, that He may send down upon all the cleansing of our transgressions.

*Sticheron 2:* Of old the Virgin and Ewe-lamb, beholding the Fruit of her womb hanged upon the Tree as one condemned, between two condemned ones, cried aloud, saying: O my Son and my God, strange to behold is this dreadful mystery! Yet the abyss of Thy wisdom can no man search out.[^1596] I hymn Thy long-suffering!

*Sticheron 3:* Where is the majesty of Thy countenance? Where hath the beauty of Thy goodness hidden itself, O Son? How is it that Thou, O God, who by Thy goodness didst create ineffable things, being fairer than the sons of men, hangest dishonored and inglorious upon the Tree for the sake of all mankind, having neither form nor comeliness, O Child?[^1597] the all-good Virgin cried aloud, groaning and weeping.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
The radiance of the luminaries set, vanquished by the noetic Light that was stripped bare upon the Cross; for the worse is ever overcome by the better, and the lesser yields place to the greater.[^1598] How then was it not fitting that, while Christ was shining forth, the sensible radiance should hide itself? Thus did the All-Pure One say unto the worthy, beholding these things, she who is the Light of the luminaries.[^1599]

*Then, "O Joyful Light"; Prokeimenon; "Vouchsafe, O Lord."*

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 2.*

*Sticheron 1:* Save me, O Christ our Savior, by the power of the Cross, Thou who didst save Peter in the sea, and have mercy on me, O God.[^1600]

*Verse:* Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes... Even so our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.[^1601]

*Sticheron 2:* "Let Him be crucified!" they cried aloud, who had ever enjoyed Thy gifts; and in place of the Benefactor, they who were murderers of the righteous asked to receive a malefactor.[^1602] But Thou didst keep silence, O Christ, enduring their cruelty, willing to suffer and to save us, as the Lover of mankind.

*Verse:* Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us... Our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.[^1603]

*Martyrikon:* The choirs of martyrs opposed the tormentors, saying: We wage warfare for the King of hosts; though ye deliver us over unto fire and torments, yet we deny not the power of the Trinity.[^1604]

*On Tuesday Evening.*
*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "When from the Tree" (Е҆гда̀ ѿ дре́ва тѧ̀):*[^1605]
Having endured many sorrows at the crucifixion of Thy Son and God, O All-Pure One, she groaned in tears and cried aloud: Woe is me, sweet Child! How is it that Thou sufferest unjustly, willing to deliver the earthborn descended from Adam? Therefore, O all-pure Virgin, we beseech thee with faith: make Him merciful unto us.

*Then, "Now lettest Thou depart"; Trisagion Prayers; after "Our Father": Troparion, Litany, and Dismissal.*[^1606]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 2.*[^1607]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Come, ye peoples, let us sing a song unto Christ God, who divided the sea and guided the people whom He brought forth from the bondage of Egypt; for He hath been glorified.[^1608]

*Refrain:* Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.

*Troparion 1:* The word of my gaining is in no wise able to hymn thee, O All-Pure One, for the darkness of my sins covers me; but O Birthgiver of God, receive me who am sorely pressed.

*Troparion 2:* Under thy protection do I flee, O All-Pure One, from my countless sins utterly; do thou make God and the Master propitious unto me, O Pure One, and save me.

*Glory:* Thou art unto me a fervent place of propitiation, O Lady; fleeing unto thee, I shall be saved, and shall obtain the salvation of my soul; for thou canst do all things, being the Mother of the God of all.

*Both now:* The deceiver of souls hath dragged me down into the pit of destruction; but stretch forth unto me thy mighty hand, O Virgin Theotokos, and swiftly lead me up unto the light.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Establish us in Thee, O Lord, who by the Tree didst put sin to death, and plant Thy fear in the hearts of us that hymn Thee.[^1609]

*Troparion 1:* Having fallen among many thieves, wretch that I am, I was stripped, wounded, and left half-dead; but do thou, O Pure Theotokos, not despise me.[^1610]

*Troparion 2:* Of old the serpent stripped Adam, having deceived him by the tree; and now he hath easily taken my soul captive. I pray thee, O Lady: have compassion on me.[^1611]

*Glory:* Now do I bring before thee my secret things, unto my true mediatress and protection, that Thy Son may not convict me of them at His righteous judgment.

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 2.*

*Both now:* Have mercy on us, O Pure One, have mercy; for from works there is no salvation for us at all. Therefore with fervent faith we cry aloud unto thee: Have mercy on thy servants!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I sing unto Thee, for by report, O Lord, have I heard of Thee and was afraid; for Thou comest unto me, seeking me that was lost. Therefore I glorify Thy great condescension toward me, O Plenteous in mercy![^1612]

*Troparion 1:* Despise me not who am incurably sick and impoverished, O Lady, but grant me the oil of thy goodness, O Virgin, and by thy providential care enrich me with that which is incorruptible gold from God.[^1613]

*Troparion 2:* I am stripped bare of all good things, and I lie in evils, clothed in them more than all who fell of old; even now adorn me with the former, and deliver me from the latter, O Bride of God.

*Glory:* I have surpassed the harlot in fornications, and the publican ever in extortion, both in word and intent; yet vouchsafe me, O Lady, before the end, to acquire the repentance of them both.[^1614]

*Both now:* I have taken up mine abode in a sorrowful wilderness, I have departed far from thee, O Birthgiver of God! Who shall give me wings, that I may fly and come unto thee, my hope, who savest from faintheartedness me the wretched?[^1615]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Giver of light and Creator of the ages, O Lord, direct us in the light of Thy commandments; for besides Thee, no other God do we know.[^1616]

*Troparion 1:* The unwaning Light shone forth from thy womb upon them that are on earth, O Lady; therefore illumine my foul soul, O Pure One, and drive away every mist from my heart.

*Troparion 2:* A cruel night of my crafty and evil deeds covers me; but I cry aloud unto thee, O Lady: Direct me unto the divine light of thy Son and Master, O All-Praised One!

*Glory:* Receive me also, O Pure One, even as Thy Son and Creator of all things received the prodigal son, crying aloud with him: Truly have I sinned; and save me, O Lady.[^1617]

*Both now:* Heal my heart, O All-Pure One, which hath been wounded by the assaults of the crafty one, as thou art compassionate, having ineffably given birth unto Him that was wounded in the flesh upon the Cross.[^1618]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 2.*

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Wallowing in the abyss of sin, I call upon the unsearchable abyss of Thy tender mercy: Bring me up from corruption, O God![^1619]

*Troparion 1:* I have fallen into the pit of sin, and seized with fear, I look not unto God who forgets not; but unto thee do I cast myself, O Bride of God.

*Troparion 2:* Guide my heart, O Virgin Theotokos, which is grievously overwhelmed by the waves of unfathomable transgressions, by thy dynamic prayer.

*Glory:* Account me worthy to behold the beauty of thy glory, O my support, O Lady, when I am about to be separated from my flesh, that thereby I may know my forgiveness.

*Both now:* Deliver thy servants from assaults, perils, and afflictions, O holy Lady, through thy prayers unto God, even us that with faith flee unto thee.

*Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:* We magnify thee, O Theotokos, crying aloud: Rejoice, O rod, from which God, having budded forth seedlessly, destroyed death upon the Tree![^1620]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* When the golden image was worshiped on the plain of Dura, Thy three youths disregarded the ungodly decree; and cast into the midst of the fire, being bedewed, they sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^1621]

*Troparion 1:* Now the enemy, gaping wide, strives to receive me into his belly; for from every side he brings against me temptations and snares, cutting off all my progress; but do thou anticipate him and deliver me, O Virgin Mother, from his crafty plot.

*Troparion 2:* The adversary defiles and disturbs my senses and my mind, and strives to bring me down into the pit of despair. Therefore I cry aloud unto thee alone: My refuge, O Bride of God, deliver me from the hand of this deceiver!

*Glory:* O my Christ, who of old didst deliver the three youths from the burning furnace: do Thou Thyself, O Word, through the intercessions of Thine unwedded Mother, deliver me and bedew me from the flame which I have kindled by my boundless evils.[^1622]

*Both now:* Finding my heart dark, the corrupter put it to death; but do thou bedew me by divine power, O unwedded Mother, and vouchsafe me to overcome him, that with faith I may call unto thee:

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 2.*

*Both now (continued):* Blessed art thou who didst bear God in the flesh!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Hymn Him as Lord, all ye works, and supreme exalt Him unto all ages: God, who descended into the fiery furnace unto the Hebrew youths, and changed the flame into dew![^1623]

*Troparion 1:* Being grievously bowed down by my many evils, I dare not lift up mine eye unto the heavens at all; but I cry aloud unto thee: Have mercy on me that am fallen, O sole Theotokos![^1624]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver me, O Pure One, from the wrath of Thy Son and God into which I have fallen; and in the hour when He shall make examination, be a help unto me, O All-Pure One, and preserve me from the goats on the left hand.[^1625]

*Glory:* Raise me up, O Maiden Bride of God, who am noetically slain by my countless evils; and by thy supplication deliver me from every deceit of the soul-corrupting enemy and murderer!

*Both now:* O soul incorrigible in evil, how hast thou wickedly blackened thy primal beauty? How hast thou rejected all thy promises unto thy Creator, and received things of malice? But come and flee devoutly unto the Theotokos!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* God the Word of God, who by ineffable wisdom came to renew Adam, who by eating had fallen grievously into corruption, and who was ineffably incarnate for our sake of the holy Virgin: Him do we the faithful with one mind magnify in songs.[^1626]

*Troparion 1:* Truly have I defiled my senses by grievous deeds, and I am full of all shameful working; but cleanse me, O All-Pure One, asking for me a time of godly compunction, that I may unceasingly magnify thee.

*Troparion 2:* Do thou justify me, O Theotokos, when the hidden deeds of every man shall appear openly; for truly thou hast the power; and deliver me from darkness, and through thy prayers establish me in the land of light, where there is joy unspeakable.[^1627]

*Glory:* I pray Thee, O Word: before the coming of the end, grant me fervent repentance, and compunction of tears, together with chastity, humility, divine love, and good ordering unto Thy flock, through the intercessions of her that bare Thee.

*Both now:* Exalted higher than both noetic and sensible creations in glory and *On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Both now (continued):* honor, next unto God, loathe me not who have surpassed in transgressions both ancient and new men who have sinned upon earth; but by thy prayer save me.

*Then, "It is truly meet"; Trisagion Prayers; and after "Our Father": Troparia and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^1628]

*On Wednesday at Matins.*
*After the First Stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 2.*[^1629]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thou hast wrought salvation in the midst of the earth, O Christ God; upon the Cross Thou didst stretch forth Thine all-pure hands, gathering all the nations who cry aloud: O Lord, glory to Thee![^1630]

*Verse:* Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet, for He is holy.[^1631]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* In like manner as the enemy took Adam captive by the tree of food, even so, O Lord, didst Thou Thyself take captive the enemy by the Tree of the Cross and by Thy Passion; for unto this didst Thou come as the second Adam, to seek him that was lost and to quicken him that was dead. O God, glory to Thee![^1632]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "Seeking the highest" (Вы́шнихъ и҆щѧ̀):*[^1633]
O new wonder! cried aloud the All-Spotless One, beholding her Son hanged upon the Cross: He who holds all things in the hollow of His hand hath submitted unto suffering; and as Judge He is condemned, granting forgiveness unto all![^1634]

*After the Second Stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 2.*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* The life-giving Cross of Thy goodness, which Thou hast bestowed upon us the unworthy, O Lord, we offer unto Thee in prayer: Save the Emperor *(name)* and Thy commonwealth; grant peace, for the sake of the Theotokos, O sole Lover of mankind.[^1635]

*Verse:* But God is our King before the ages; He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.[^1636]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Thou who didst enlighten earthly things by the Cross, and called sinners unto repentance, separate me not from Thy flock, O Good Shepherd, but seek me who am lost, O Master, and number me with Thy flock of a hundred, O Thou who alone art good and the Lover of mankind.[^1637]

*Verse:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.[^1638]

*Martyrikon:* O Thou who hast illumined Thy saints more than gold, and glorified Thy venerable ones as Thou art good, being entreated by them, O Christ *On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Martyrikon (continued):* God, pacify our life as the Lover of mankind, and direct our prayer as incense, O Thou who alone restest in the saints.[^1639]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Милосе́рдїѧ сꙋ́щи):*[^1640]
The Virgin and Thy Mother, O Christ, beholding Thee stretched out dead upon the Cross, weeping bitterly, said: O my Son, what is this dreadful mystery? How is it that Thou, who bestowest eternal life upon all, diest voluntarily upon the Cross an ignominious death?[^1641]

*After the Third Stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 2.*
*Tone 2, Podoben: "Being the wellspring of loving-kindness" (Милосе́рдїѧ сꙋ́щи):*[^1642]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Like the thief do I confess, and I cry aloud unto Thee, O Good One: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom, and number me with him, O Thou who voluntarily didst accept passions for our sake.[^1643]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Thou didst endure to be crucified for our sake, and to be dead, O Jesus, Giver of life, that Thou mightest deliver the creation of Thy hands from mortal condemnation, as Thou art a compassionate God and Lover of mankind, according to the multitude of Thy tender mercies, O Thou who alone art sinless.

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Preserved by the precious Cross of Thy Son, O Lady, pure Theotokos, we all easily vanquish every assault of the adversary; therefore, as is our duty, we call thee blessed as the Mother of God, the sole hope of our souls.

*Canon to the Precious and Life-Giving Cross, whose acrostic is: "The standing of the Cross is the downfall of demons." By Joseph. Tone 2.*[^1644]

*Ode 1. Heirmos:* The overwhelmingly armed power of old laid low the entire host of Pharaoh in the deep; and the incarnate Word, the supremely glorified Lord, hath blotted out all-wicked sin; for gloriously hath He been glorified.[^1645]

*Refrain:* Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.

*Troparion 1:* Having transgressed the first commandment of old, the first-created found death from the garden; but the Immortal One, raised upon the Tree and having tasted of death, bestowed immortality upon all men.[^1646]

*Troparion 2:* When the Cross was planted on earth, the haughtiness of the enemy fell down and was utterly destroyed; and man, who was formerly cast out, enters again into Paradise. Glory to Thee, our God, who alone hast thus been well-pleased![^1647]

*Martyrikon:* Like rams, like noetic lambs, being all slaughtered and mercilessly cut into pieces, O most praised martyrs, ye were offered *On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Martyrikon (continued):* unto Him that was slain, even as sheep; and now do ye enlighten the holy Church of the firstborn.[^1648]

*Martyrikon 2:* Ever making a steadfast stand, O wise ones, ye cast down the crafty enemies, being cut down by swords, and cast into fire and into water; therefore have ye been accounted worthy of a blessed end, O passion-bearers.[^1649]

*Theotokion:* The sacred choir of the prophets called thee the closed gate, the chosen land, and the unhewn mountain, O all-pure Lady; for thou didst bear the Master of all, who voluntarily accepted crucifixion in the flesh.[^1650]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 2.*[^1651]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Come, ye peoples, let us sing a song unto Christ God, who divided the sea and guided the people whom He brought forth from the bondage of Egypt; for He hath been glorified.[^1652]

*Troparion 1:* Come, all ye faithful, let us with one accord hymn the Theotokos; for she bare Christ beyond the mind of man, and unceasingly prays to save us all.

*Troparion 2:* He who is the express Image of the Father's hypostasis took from thee our mortal nature, O Birthgiver of God, and glorified the image that was corrupted, and made it new.[^1653]

*Troparion 3:* Being more honorable than the Cherubim, thou hast become higher than the heavenly spheres; for beyond human mind didst thou dispassionately contain God within thy womb.[^1654]

*Troparion 4:* As redemption from our sins didst thou bear the Lord and Benefactor, and having changed the deadness of forefather Adam, thou hast raised our nature unto the heavenly realms.

*Canon of the Cross, Ode 3. Heirmos:* The wilderness hath blossomed like a lily, O Lord, even the barren Church of the Gentiles by Thy coming, wherein my heart hath been established.[^1655]

*Troparion 1:* Being crucified, Thou didst shake all creation, O Lord, and didst establish the faithful who hymn Thy power and Thine ineffable condescension, O Word.

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast opened Paradise by Thy Cross, O Master, and didst lead in the thief who recognized Thy kingdom and the wealth of Thy divine tender compassion.[^1656]

*Martyrikon 1:* The martyrs blossomed forth like fragrant roses in the noetic valleys, and *On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Martyrikon 1 (continued):* drove away foul-smelling deceit, and filled the hearts of the faithful with fragrance.

*Martyrikon 2:* O luminaries of the inhabited earth, holy protectors of the faithful, with radiant beams of the Spirit enlighten all who dutifully call you blessed.

*Theotokion:* Beholding the Giver of life lifted up upon the Tree, voluntarily dying, He who bestows life upon all, the All-Spotless One was afflicted in her womb.

*Another Heirmos:* Establish us in Thee, O Lord, who by the Tree didst put sin to death, and plant Thy fear in the hearts of us that hymn Thee.[^1657]

*Troparion 1:* As truly the golden censer, and the jar of manna, and the mountain of God, and the fair divine palace do we name thee, O Virgin.[^1658]

*Troparion 2:* Being the temple and sacred dwelling of the Word, O Theotokos, be thou unto me a place of cleansing for transgressions, O all-pure Ever-Virgin!

*Troparion 3:* Neither tongue of the earthborn nor mind of the bodiless hosts is able to recount thy childbirth; for beyond nature and understanding, O Theotokos, didst thou bear the Creator.

*Troparion 4:* Be a confirmation and refuge and protection, O Virgin Birthgiver of God, unto them that flee unto thee with faith and proclaim thee to be the Mother of God.

*Canon of the Cross, Ode 4. Heirmos:* Thou hast come forth from the Virgin, not an ambassador nor an angel, but Thyself incarnate, O Lord, and hast saved the whole of me, a man; therefore I cry aloud unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^1659]

*Troparion 1:* Being lifted up upon the Tree, Thou didst destroy the dominion of the cruel ruler of the world, and didst blot out the curse; therefore we that are saved through Thee glorify Thee, O sole Lord.[^1660]

*Troparion 2:* Beholding Thee stretched out upon the Tree, the sun hid its light, the mountains and the rocks were split, and the veil of the temple was rent, O Almighty One.[^1661]

*Martyrikon 1:* Being put to death, ye put the enemy to death utterly; being scraped with iron, ye stripped off the grossness of bodily mortality, O martyrs of the Lord, and were clothed with glory.[^1662]

*Martyrikon 2:* Being beheaded, the martyrs dreadfully cut off the heads of the crafty powers, and rejoicing, inherited the enduring glory.

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Theotokion:* Christ, the Father's Radiance, shone forth from thy womb, O unwedded Maiden, and being crucified, enlightened the universe, and destroyed the demonic darkness.[^1663]

*Another Heirmos, the same.*
*Troparion 1:* Rain down upon me a drop of compunction, O Lady, taking away all the burning heat of my heart, and staying the turbid torrents of my thoughts.[^1664]

*Troparion 2:* Despise me not who am wounded by the weapon of pleasures and lie afflicted, O All-Pure One, but heal me by the spear and the Blood of Thy crucified Son and our God.[^1665]

*Troparion 3:* Having enriched all creation with dominion, have compassion with divine grace on me who am grievously impoverished, that I may magnify thee as my good mediatress, O All-Spotless One.

*Troparion 4:* Refresh me with repentance, O Virgin, and bedew my heart that is dried up by the heat of passions, pouring out upon me the oil and healing of thy mercy.

*Canon of the Cross, Ode 5. Heirmos:* Thou hast become a mediator between God and men, O Christ God; for through Thee, O Master, have we access from the night of ignorance unto Thine all-radiant Father.[^1666]

*Troparion 1:* Being nailed unto the Cross, Thou didst shake the foundations of the earth; and being pierced with the spear, Thou didst slay the dragon, the originator of evil, and didst pour forth streams of salvation unto all, O Christ.[^1667]

*Troparion 2:* Enduring not to behold him wandering whom Thou hadst fashioned with Thine hands, Thou didst stretch out Thy hands, O Word, being put to death upon the Tree; and Thou didst quicken him who of old had died through the tree.

*Martyrikon 1:* Friends of Christ, fervent protectors of men, precious flowers, adorned vessels of the Spirit, the wise passion-bearers are worthily called blessed.

*Martyrikon 2:* Having lawfully contended, the choir of Thy holy sufferers put the lawless to shame, O sole Lawgiver, by Thy power; and having finished their course, they were crowned as martyrs.[^1668]

*Theotokion:* Thou hast appeared incorrupt after childbirth, O Pure One; for God was born of thee, taking flesh; whom beholding crucified, unable to bear it, thou didst cry aloud in the grief of thy womb.[^1669]

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Another Heirmos:* O Giver of light and Creator of the ages, O Lord: guide us in the light of Thy commandments; for we know no other God save Thee.[^1670]

*Troparion 1:* Knowing Thee to be incarnate of thee, O Birthgiver of God, we the faithful acknowledge the Son born without seed, true God, and man by nature; therefore do we glorify thee.[^1671]

*Troparion 2:* Having recourse at all times with faith beneath thy shelter and protection, O All-Pure One, for thy sake, O Virgin, are we delivered from every cruel circumstance.

*Troparion 3:* Deliver us from temptations and from the tempest of thoughts, O All-Pure One: from all anger and every sin, from famine and destruction, and from eternal torment, O Virgin.

*Troparion 4:* Being our protectress, salvation, and hope, O Sovereign Lady of Christians: by love save them that ever faithfully hymn thee, O all-hymned Virgin.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* From the whale Jonah cried unto the Lord: Do Thou lead me up from the depths of hades, I pray, that as unto a deliverer I may sacrifice unto Thee in the voice of praise and the spirit of truth.[^1672]

*Troparion 1:* In times past, Jacob laid his hands upon the youthful heads, depicting beforehand the Cross, upon which, O Word, Thou didst stretch out Thy palms, and didst deliver mankind from the hand of the deceitful wrestler, O Christ.[^1673]

*Troparion 2:* When Thou, O Christ, the King of all, wast crucified voluntarily, reigning sin was abolished; and Adam, who of old was driven forth from Paradise, is settled therein again, hymning Thee.[^1674]

*Martyrikon 1:* Being fair in their sacred wounds, who were adorned with celestial majesty: let us with unhesitating heart hymn the truly beloved martyrs of the Lord.[^1675]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having kept their lamps unquenched, the divine martyrs abundantly fed them with their blood, and rejoicing, obtained entrance into the divine bridal chamber.[^1676]

*Theotokion:* We hymn thee, O All-Hymned One, as having given birth unto the all-praised God, who destroyed upon the Tree the adversary foe, and delivered from corruption them that hymn His sufferings.[^1677]

*Another Heirmos:* Wallowing in the abyss of sin, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy loving-kindness: Lead me up from corruption, O God.[^1678]

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 1:* He who by His will made all things was pleased to dwell within the womb of her that knew not wedlock, and being compassionate, enriched with incorruption them that were ailing through corruption.[^1679]

*Troparion 2:* Higher than the heavenly hosts and more holy art thou, O All-Immaculate One, having contained beyond nature within thy womb the uncontainable Word.[^1680]

*Troparion 3:* Lead me into the path of repentance, O Sovereign Lady, who wander upon the path of life and frequently fall into the sinful byways.

*Troparion 4:* Despise not, O Pure One, the entreaties of thy servants who place their hope in thee; for thou art a refuge, O Sovereign Lady, and a cleansing place for our souls.

*Canon of the Cross, Ode 7. Heirmos:* The God-opposing decree of the lawless tyrant raised a high flame; but Christ spread a spiritual dew upon the pious children: He who is blessed and supreme in glory.[^1681]

*Troparion 1:* The sword which turned about of old now gives way unto me, since Thou, O Compassionate Lord, being lifted up upon the Cross, wast pierced with the spear; therefore I magnify Thee, having found dispassion through Thy precious Passion.[^1682]

*Troparion 2:* The serpent lifted up upon the tree by Moses prefigured the divine elevation of Christ, who put to death the deceitful serpent, and quickened all of us who were slain through transgression.[^1683]

*Martyrikon 1:* Ye became sons of the beginningless Father through divine communion, and of the co-beginningless Son, O holy ones, having been conformed unto His all-pure Passion; therefore He calls you brethren and heirs of His kingdom.[^1684]

*Martyrikon 2:* Being lifted up upon crosses like the Master, pierced with spears, cut down by swords, cast into fire and water, and crushed upon wheels, the God-bearing martyrs rejoiced.[^1685]

*Theotokion:* Beholding hanging upon the Tree the ripened Cluster, whom thou, O Pure One, didst rear without cultivation, thou didst cry aloud: O my sweet Child, drop down sweetness, whereby the drunkenness of passions shall be taken away![^1686]

*Another Heirmos, the same:* Being my strength and song and salvation, my certain protection, and an insurmountable wall, O Sovereign Lady, subdue the demons that wage war against me, *On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Another Heirmos (continued):* who ever seek to put me to death. *(Twice)*

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst incarnate God from thy virginal blood, who deified mankind, O Virgin; therefore by thy prayers deliver me who am defiled by passions and corrupted by the wiles of the enemy.[^1687]

*Troparion 2:* The furnace prefigured thy childbirth, O All-Immaculate One; for it burned not the youths, even as the unapproachable fire burned not thy womb; therefore we pray thee: Deliver thy servants from eternal fire.[^1688]

*Canon of the Cross, Ode 8. Heirmos:* Of old the fiery furnace in Babylon divided its operation by the command of God, consuming the Chaldeans, but bedewing the faithful who sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^1689]

*Troparion 1:* By the blood which dropped from Thy pure ribs, O Long-suffering One, creation was sanctified, and the rivers of polytheism were dried up, and clouds of piety appeared, destroying the drought of delusion.[^1690]

*Troparion 2:* The sun was abashed at Thy crucifixion and hid its rays, the rocks were split, hades below was terrified, and the spirits of the righteous leaped for joy, expecting total deliverance, O Word.[^1691]

*Martyrikon 1:* The relics of the passion-bearers pour forth healings unto the faithful, and ever cure the wounds of intractable diseases. Truly marvelous art Thou, O Lord, we cry in thanksgiving, in Thy holy martyrs![^1692]

*Martyrikon 2:* The mouths of beasts, the mire of stalls, freezing cold and frost, the weight of burning heat, the tearing of nails, imprisonments, and violent death did ye endure with steadfastness, O passion-bearers! Therefore are ye glorified with Christ.[^1693]

*Theotokion:* He who wills to do so accepts flesh from thy womb, and the Invisible One becomes visible; He accepts crucifixion, and is called a curse, delivering all from the curse, even Thy Son, O Lady full of the grace of God.[^1694]

*Another Heirmos:* Let us hymn Him who of old bedewed the Hebrew youths in the flame, and gloriously burned up the Chaldeans therein, even the Lord, saying: Bless and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^1695]

*Troparion 1:* A living and ungrudging salvation for us the earthborn, O immaculate Ewe-lamb, *On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 2.*

*Troparion 1 (continued):* who didst bring forth the Lamb of God: despise me not who cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^1696]

*Troparion 2:* Thy divine childbirth, O All-Pure One, hath renewed us, and shown all to be children and sons of the day and of light; saved through whom, we cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^1697]

*Troparion 3:* Thou didst bring forth living water from thy virginal womb, O Pure One, and didst pour forth forgiveness from fountains of healing for the faithful; therefore we all cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^1698]

*Troparion 4:* Thou, O Pure One, hast grown the ripe Cluster of life; for thou art the vine that sweetens the earth with good things; hymning which we cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.
